itu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil uyireḻuttukkaḷil pattāvatu; aṅkāttalōṭu itaḻ kuvitalāṉpiṟakkum uyireḻuttuk kaḷuḷ oṉṟu; tamiḻc ciṟappeḻuttu aintaṉuḷ oṉṟu; ōkāra neṭilukku iṉamāṉa kuṟil; ōkāram aḷapeṭukku miṭattu ataṉai aṭuttu varuvatu; nakaraoṟṟu oṉṟuṭaṉēyē kūṭi (no - eṉa) moḻiyiṟutiyil varuvatu; muṉṉilai ēva lorumai viṉaiyākiya ō eṉpatu aḷapeṭukkumiṭattum, ciṟappup poruḷil varum ōkāram aḷapeṭukkumiṭattum, moḻiyiṟutiyil okaram aḷapeṭai yeḻuttāka nikaḻum.
okara īṟṟup puṇarcci -
muṉṉilai moḻikkaṇ varum okaraīṟu vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : ōok koṟṟā; ō - ‘iṅṅaṉam ceytalai oḻi’ eṉṉum poruḷatu.	(to. e. 272 nac.)
ciṟappup poruḷil varum okaraīṟu vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	 nīyōo koṭiyai	(273 nac.)
okaram puḷḷi peṟutal -
‘ekaram puḷḷi peṟutal’ kāṇka.
okaram moḻiyīṟātal -
okaram nakarameyyuṭaṉ kūṭiyē ‘no’ eṉa moḻiyīṟām. atu vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇartalē perum pāṉmai; meṉkaṇam variṉ meli mikutalum ām.
	e-ṭu :	no koṟṟā - itu viṭṭicaittaliṉ valleḻuttu mikātāyiṟṟu.
non nākā, nom māṭā - eṉa melleḻuttu mikkum
no nākā, no māṭā - eṉa viṭṭicaittu iyalpākavum varum.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar nok koṟṟā  eṉa valleḻuttu mikum eṉṟār. ōreḻuttorumoḻi muṉṉilai viṉaiccol mikkē muṭital koḷka eṉṟār avar.	(to. e. 151 nac.)
ō eṉṟa muṉṉilai ēvalviṉai, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam vantaviṭattu aḷapeṭuttu valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyumvaḻi, okaram nilaimoḻi yīṟṟil varum.	(272 nac.)
e-ṭu : ōok koṟṟā
ciṟappup poruḷil varum ōkāra iṭaiccol aḷapeṭuttu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpāka muṭiyum.	(273 nac.)
e-ṭu : yāṉōo koṭiyaṉ
oṭṭiya oṟṟu iṭai mikutal -
oṭṭiya oṟṟāvatu nilaimoḻi īṟṟeḻuttai oṭṭi varumoḻi mutalil varum uyirmeyeḻuttiṉkaṇ uḷḷa meyyeḻuttu.
nilaimoḻiyākum a i u - eṉṟa mūṉṟu cuṭṭiṭaic coṟkaḷum varumoḻi meṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumvaḻit tamakkup poruntiya oṟṟukkaḷāka varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ varum oṟṟukkaḷ mikkup puṇartal.
e-ṭu :	aññāṇ,  annūl, ammaṇi - to.e. 205 nac.
	iññāṇ, innūl, immaṇi - to.e.238 nac.
	uññāṇ, unnūl, ummaṇi - 256 nac.
oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakku -
oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkāvatu coṟkaḷ cāriyai peṟutaṟku ēṟṟa moḻiyamaippām. ākavē ellāc coṟkaḷum cāriyai peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa varaiyaṟai iṉṟu. mēlum iṉṉa iṉṉa coṟkaḷ iṉṉa iṉṉa cāriyai peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa varaiyaṟaiyum uṇṭu. ivvarai yaṟai cāṉṟōr vaḻakkum, cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷum nōkkik koḷḷappaṭṭatām.
nilā eṉpatu attuccāriyai peṟum eṉṟa vitiyai (e. 228 nac.), (varumoḻi peyarāy vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci ākiyaviṭattu) oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakku aṉmaiyiṉ, nilākkatir - nilāmuṟṟam - eṉpaṉa peṟāvāyiṉa.	(to.e. 132 nac.)
oṭṭuppeyar -
pala coṟkaḷ oṭṭi niṉṟu oru peyaraik kuṟikka varuvatu oṭṭuppeyarām. pakāppatam iraṇṭu mutal ēḻeḻuttu īṟākavum, pakupatam iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu eḻuttu īṟākavum toṭarum. āṉāl kaṅkai koṇṭa cōḻapuram, irata nūpuraccakkaravāḷam (cūḷā. irata.12.), pāṇṭiyaṉ pal yākacālai mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti - eṉṟal toṭakkattu oṭṭuppeyarkku varaiyaṟai illai eṉka.(naṉ. 129 mayilai.)

oṭu eṉṟa marappeyar puṇarumāṟu -
oṭu eṉṟa marappeyar vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ, uti eṉṟa marappeyar pōla, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ otta melleḻuttu iṭaiyē mikku muṭiyum.
e-ṭu : oṭuṅkōṭu, oṭuñcetiḷ, oṭuntōl, oṭumpū
ciṟupāṉmai oṭuvaṅkōṭu - eṉa, ammuccāriyai iṭaiyē peṟutalum uṇṭu.	(to. e. 262 nac. urai)
otta oṟṟu mikal -
varumoḻi mutalil ka ca ta pa - eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ variṉ, avvoṟṟukkaḷē nilaimoḻi varumoḻikaḷukku iṭaiyē mikutal.
e-ṭu : viḷakkuṟitu, viḷacciṟitu, viḷattītu, viḷapperitu
eṉavē, nilaimoḻiyīṟum varumoḻi valliṉamutalum puṇarumvaḻi, iṭaiyē varum valloṟṟu varumoḻi mutalil varum valloṟṟēyām.  avai iyaipu valleḻuttu eṉappaṭum.	(to. e. 203 nac.)
otta kuṟṟeḻuttu -
uyirneṭilkaḷukku iṉamāka varum avvak kuṟṟeḻut tukkaḷ. ākārattukku akaramum, īkārattukku ikaramum, ūkārattukku ukaramum, ēkārattukku ekaramum, ōkārat tukku okaramum otta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷām. aikārattukku ikaramum, oḷakārattukku ukaramum aḷapeṭaiyāy icai niṟaikkum otta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ.	(to. e. 41, 42 nac.)
orupaḵtu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ muṉ ēṉaiya eṇkaḷ -
orupaḵtu mutalāya eṭṭu eṇkaḷ nilaimoḻiyāka niṟpa, varu moḻiyākaoṉṟu mutal oṉpatu eṇkaḷum avai yūrnta piṟapeyarum varumāyiṉ, nilaimoḻiyiṉ āytam keṭat takara oṟṟu aṅku varum.
	varumāṟu : orupaḵtu + oṉṟu ḻū orupattu + oṉṟu = orupat toṉṟu; eṇpaḵtu + oṉpatu ḻūeṇpattu + oṉpatu = eṇpattoṉpatu; irupaḵtu + mūṉṟu kalam ḻū irupattu + mūṉṟukalam = irupattu mūṉṟukalam.
orupatu mutaliyavaṟṟukkum ippuṇarcci okkum.
orupatu + oṉṟu = orupattoṉṟu - eṉavarum.	(naṉ. 196)
orupaḵtu mutaliyavai urupēṟkumpōtu aṭaiyum tiripukaḷ -
orupaḵtu mutaliyaṉa urupēṟkumiṭattu iṭaiyē āṉ cāriyai vara, paḵtu eṉpataṉ pakara oṟṟu nīṅkalāka ‘aḵtu’ eṉpatu keṭa, āṉcāriyai peṟṟu, orupāṉai, irupāṉai, muppāṉai, nāṟpāṉai, aimpāṉai, aṟupāṉai, eḻu pāṉai, eṇpāṉai - eṉa muṭiyum.	(to.e.199 nac.)
potuvitiyāṉ eṇṇuppeyar aṉcāriyai peṟum marapai oṭṭi, orupaḵtu + aṉ + ai = orupaḵtaṉai...... eṇpaḵtaṉai eṉa muṭitalumuṇṭu.	( 199 nac. urai)
oṉpaḵtu eṉpatum paḵtu eṉa muṭiyum collātaliṉ, atuvum oṉpāṉai, oṉpaḵtaṉai - eṉṟu urupēṟkum  muṭivu koḷḷum.	( 199 nac. urai)
orupaḵtu mutaliyavai niṟai aḷavup peyarkaḷoṭu puṇarumāṟu -
orupaḵtu muṉṉar niṟaippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum varuvaḻi, orupatiṉkaḻañcu, orupatiṉkalam, orupatiṟṟukkaḻañcu, orupatiṟṟukkalam- eṉa ‘iṉ’ peṟṟum iṉ ‘iṟṟu’ ākiyum puṇarum.
immuṭipai orupaḵtu mutal eṇpaḵtu muṭiyavum koḷka.
eṇpatiṉkalam, eṇpatiṟṟukkalam - eṉap puṇarkka.
varumoḻi poruṭpeyarāyavaḻiyum patiṟṟuvēli, patiṟṟut toṭi- eṉṟāṟ pōla muṭivaṉavum koḷka.	(to. e. 436 nac.)
uyir varuvaḻip patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku - eṉa iṉ ‘iṟṟu’ ākum eṉka.	(121 nac. urai)
orupatu mutaliyavai urupoṭu puṇartal  -
orupatu,  irupatu..... eṇpatu - eṉṉum eṭṭu eṇkaḷ nilai moḻiyāka niṟka, urupu vantu puṇarumiṭattu, iṭaiyē āṉcāriyai varutalumuṇṭu; vārāmaiyumuṇṭu. atu varumiṭattu nilaimoḻiyil ‘patu’ eṉpataṉkaṇ pakara oṟṟu nīṅkalākap piṟa keṭum. oṉpatu eṉṟa nilaimoḻikkum itu poruntum. orupaḵtu irupaḵtu.... oṉpaḵtu - eṉa āytam peṟiṉum ivviti poruntum.
e-ṭu: 	orupatu, orupaḵtu+ aiḻū orup + āṉ + ai = oru pāṉai; oṉpatu, oṉpaḵtu +ai ḻū oṉp +  āṉ+ ai = oṉpāṉai.	orupatu, orupaḵtu + ai = orupatai, oru paḵtai; oṉpatu, oṉpaḵtu + ai =  oṉpatai, oṉ paḵtai eṉac cāriyai peṟātu; muṭintaṉa.	(naṉ.249)
oru puṇarccikkaṇ muttiripum varutal -
makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ - maka(m)+ attu+ āṉ+ koṇṭāṉ; ippuṇarccikkaṇ, attum āṉum mikutal; attiṉ akaram keṭutal; āṉ cāriyaiyiṉ ṉakara oṟṟu ṟakara oṟṟāka mey piṟitu ātal.
ivvāṟu oru puṇarccikkaṇṇēyē, meypiṟitātal - mikutal - kuṉṟal - eṉṟa muttiripukaḷum vantaṉa.	(to. e. 109 nac.)
e-ṭu :	yāṉai + kōṭu  = yāṉaikkōṭu - vali mikal; nilam + paṉai =  nilappaṉai - makaram keṭutal, pakarammikal; paṉai + kāy = paṉaṅkāy -  ai keṭutal, ‘am’mikal, ammiṉ  makaram ṅakaramākat tirital. ivvāṟu oru puṇarcciyil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu vikāramum varutal kāṇka.	(naṉ. 157)
orumoḻi ilakkaṇam -
‘āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār orumoḻi ilakkaṇam kūṟāmai’ kāṇka.
olvaḻi oṟṟu mikutal -
nilaimoḻikaḷai aṭuttu valleḻuttai mutalāvatākak koṇṭa ku - kaṇ - eṉṟa vēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷ puṇariṉ, poruntumiṭattu valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyiṉum mikum. ciṟupāṉmai iyalpātalum, ciṟupāṉmai nilai moḻi īṟutiritalum uḷa.
e-ṭu: maṇikku, maṇikkaṇ; īkku, īkkaṇ; tiṉaikku, tiṉaikkaṇ; vēykku, vēykkaṇ; vērkku, vērk kaṇ; vīḻkku, vīḻkkaṇ - ivai valloṟṟumikkaṉa. valloṟṟu mikum nilaimoḻi īṟukaḷ i, ī, ai, y, r, ḻ - eṉṟa āṟākum.
tām, nām - eṉpaṉa kaṇṇurupu varumiṭattu, neṭumutal kuṟuki makaram keṭṭu ṅakara melloṟṟu mikuvaṉavām.
taṅkaṇ, naṅkaṇ - eṉa varum.
uyartiṇaippeyar viravuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉ kaṇurupu iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	nampikaṇ, naṅkaikaṇ, aracarkaṇ;
			tampikaṇ, tantaikaṇ, tāykaṇ - eṉa varum.
uyartiṇaippeyar viravuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉ kuvvurupu mikkup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	nampikku, naṅkaikku, aracarkku;
			tampikku, tantaikku, tāykku - eṉa varum.
iṅṅaṉam kaṇurupu varuvaḻi iyalpākum nilaimoḻi īṟukaḷ i, ai, y, r - eṉpaṉa.
vēṟku, vēṟkaṇ; vāṭku, vāṭkaṇ - eṉa lakara ḷakara īṟukaḷtirintaṉa.	(to. e. 144 nac. urai)
‘ovvoṉṟu koṭu’: taḻāt toṭar -
‘ovvoṉṟu koṭu’ eṉṟāl, immuṟaiyē palavaṟṟaiyum koṭu eṉap poruḷtantu niṟṟaliṉ, ‘ovvoṉṟu’ eṉpatu aṭukkaṉṟu; vēṟṟumai allaṉa ellām alvaḻi ātaliṉ, taḻāttoṭarāy alvaḻiyuḷ oṉṟām eṉpatu. 	(naṉ. 199 caṅkara.)
oḻintataṉ nilai -
oḻiyicai ōkārattiṉ nilai. ōkāra iṭaiccol cuṭṭum pala poruḷkaḷil oḻiyicaip poruḷum oṉṟu. ēṉaiya ōkāraṅkaḷai aṭutta nilaimoḻiyīṟukaḷ varumoḻi vaṉ kaṇattōṭu iyalpākap puṇarvatu pōlavē, oḻiyicai ōkāramum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	koḷalō koṇṭāṉ . (koṇṭu uyyakkoṇ ṭāṉ allaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ.); celalō ceṉṟāṉ (ceṉṟu uyyac ceṉṟāṉ allaṉ.); taralō tantāṉ (tantu uyyat tantāṉ allaṉ); pōtalō pōntāṉ (pōntu  uyyap pōntāṉ allaṉ) - ivai iyalpākap puṇarntaṉa.	(to. e. 291 nac. urai)
oṟṟaḷapeṭai -
ṅñ ṇ n m ṉ v y l ḷ ḵ - eṉṉum patiṉoru puḷḷiyum kuṟiṟkīḻum kuṟiliṇaikkīḻum aḷapeṭukkum, eṉṉai?
‘vaṉmaiyoṭu raḵkāṉ ḻaḵkāṉ oḻittāṅku
almey āytamōṭu aḷapeḻum orōvaḻi’
eṉpa vākaliṉ.
	varalāṟu : maṅṅkalam, maññcu, maṇṇṇu, panntu, ammpu, miṉṉṉu, tevvvar, meyyyar, vellka, koḷḷka, eḵḵku - ivai kuṟiṟkīḻ aḷa peḻuntaṉa.
araṅṅkam, muraññcu, muraṇṇṭu, marunntu, arummpu, muraṉṉṟu, kuravvvai, arayyyar, kurallkaḷ, tiraḷḷkaḷ, varaḵḵku - ivai kuṟiliṇaikkīḻ aḷapeḻuntaṉa.	(nēmi. eḻut. 3 urai)
ceyyuṭkaṇ ōcaiyai niṟaippataṟku ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa va ya la ḷa - eṉṟa pattu meyyeḻuttukkaḷum āytamum, kuṟilkīḻum kuṟiliṇaikkīḻum, moḻi yiṭaiyiṉum iṟutiyilum tam māttiraiyiṉ mikku orumāttiraiyāka olippatu oṟṟaḷa peṭaiyām. av voṟṟu aḷapeṭuttamai tōṉṟa atuvē aṭuttu varivaṭivil aṟikuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭum.
	e-ṭu : 
‘naṅṅ kaḷaṅkaṟuppām nām’
‘ilaṅṅku veṇpiṟaicū ṭīcaṉ aṭiyārkku’
‘veḵḵku vārkkillai vīṭu’
‘ilaḵḵku muttiṉ iṉam’
patiṉoru puḷḷiyeḻuttukkaḷum kuṟilkīḻum kuṟiliṇak kīḻum colliṉ iṭaiyum iṟutiyumākiya nāṉkiṭattum aḷapeṭuppavē, varum oṟṟaḷapeṭai 44 ākum. āytam moḻiyiṭaik kaṇaṉṟi vārāmaiyiṉ, iṟutikkaṇ iraṇṭu nilaikaḷaiyum ataṟku nīkka oṟṟaḷapeṭai 42ām.	(naṉ. 92)
oṟṟaḷapeṭai cārpeḻuttātal  -
oru māttiraiyāy alaku peṟutalāṉum piṟavāṟṟāṉum oṟṟaḷapeṭai cārpeḻuttu eṉa, oṟṟiṉ vēṟāyiṟṟu.			(i.vi. 20 urai)
oṟṟaḷapeṭai tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟappaṭāmai -
oṟṟaḷapeṭai ceyyuṭkē varutaliṉ, eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟappaṭāmal, poruḷatikārattuc ceyyuḷiyalil kūṟap paṭṭatu. (āyiṉ ‘oṟṟicai nīṭal’ (e.33 nac.) eṉa eḻuttati kārattil atu cuṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu)	(to. e. 6 nac. urai)
oṟṟu icai nīṭal -
meyyeḻuttukkaḷ tamakkuriya araimāttiraiyiṉ nīṇ ṭolittal. oṟṟukkaḷ icainūliṉpaṭi patiṉoru māttiraiyaḷavu nīṇṭicaikkum eṉpa. oṟṟukkaḷ nīṇ ṭolikkumiṭattu oṟṟaḷapeṭai eṉṟu peyar peṟum. ōr oṟṟu oli nīḷumpōtu aṭuttu eḻutappaṭum atē oṟṟu oṟṟaḷapeṭai eḻuttākum.	(to. e. 33 nac.)
oṟṟutalum varuṭutalum -
oṟṟutal taṭṭutal eṉavum aḻuttit taṭavutal eṉavum poruḷ peṟum. varuṭutal mellat taṭavutal eṉṟu poruḷ peṟum.
nānuṉi mēlnōkkic ceṉṟu mēlvāyai oṟṟutalāl ṟa ṉa -vum, nānuṉi mēlnōkkic ceṉṟu mēlvāyai varuṭuta lāl ra ḻa - vum piṟakkum. eṉavē, varuṭutalāl iṭainikarāṉa oliyum, oṟṟutalāl aḻuttamāṉa oliyum piṟakkum eṉpatu. (ṟa ṉa ra ḻa: meyyeḻuttaik kuṟippaṉa.)	(to. e. 94, 95 nac.)
oṉ cāriyai -
ōkāra īṟṟup peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumaip puṇarccikkaṇ orōvaḻi oṉcāriyai peṟum. oṉcāriyai piṟkālattu ‘ṉ’ cāriyai āyiṟṟu.
	e-ṭu :	kō+ oṉ + kai = kōoṉkai -	(to.e.294 nac.)
		kō+ oṉ + ai = kōoṉai	(180 nac.)
oṉpatu ‘nūṟu’ eṉpataṉoṭu puṇartal  -
oṉpatu eṉpataṉ okarattoṭu takaram cēra, (‘patu’ keṭa) ṉakaram ḷakaramāki iraṭṭa, varumoḻi nūṟu ‘āyiram’ ākat tiriyat toḷḷāyiram- eṉa muṭiyum.
oṉpatu + nūṟu ḻū toṉpatu + nūṟu ḻū toṉ+ nūṟu ḻū
toḷḷ + nūṟu ḻū toḷḷ +āyiram =  toḷḷāyiram
(to. e. 463 nac.)
oṉpatu ‘paḵtu’ eṉpataṉoṭu puṇartal  -
nilaimoḻi oṉpatu;varumoḻi paḵtu. nilaimoḻi okarattoṭu takaraoṟṟuc cēra (nilaimoḻi ‘patu’ keṭa), ṉakaram ṇakaramāki iraṭṭa, varumoḻiyiṉkaṇ āytamum pakaramum keṭa, ūkāram varat tukaram ṟukaramākat tiriya, nilaimoḻi ṇakarattiṉmēl ūkāram ēṟi muṭiyat toṇṇūṟu ākum.
oṉpatu+ paḵtu ḻū toṉpatu+ paḵtu ḻū toṉ+ paḵtu ḻū toṇṇ + paḵtu ḻū toṇṇ+ ūṟu= toṇṇūṟu. 	(to. e. 445. nac.)
‘oṉpatum iṉaittē’ -
oṉpatu eṉpataṉ īṟṟuyirmeyyaik keṭātu kuṟṟukara īṟṟup potuvitiyāṉ uyir oṉṟumē keṭa, iṉṉum iṟṟum ēṟpatu ēṟṟu niṟṟaliṉ ‘oṉpatum iṟṟē’ eṉat taṉmaiyaṇi ākkātu, uvamaiyaṇi ākki ‘iṉaittē’ eṉṟār. oṉpatu eṉpataṉ iṟuti pattu eṉpatu pōla muraṇi niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉōṭu itu māṭṭeṟiyap paṭṭatu.
	varumāṟu : oṉpatu+ āyiram ḻū oṉpat +iṉ + āyiram = oṉpatiṉāyiram
		oṉpatu + oṉṟu ḻū oṉpat + iṟṟu + oṉṟu = oṉpatiṟṟoṉṟu	(naṉ. 197 caṅkara.)
oṉpatuvakai mayakkam -
uyirōṭu uyir, uyiroṭu mey, uyirōṭu uyirmey, meyyoṭu mey, meyyōṭu uyir, meyyōṭu uyirmey, uyirmeyyōṭu uyirmey, uyirmeyyōṭu uyir, uyir meyyoṭu mey - eṉpaṉa oṉpatu vakai mayakkaṅkaḷām. ivaṟṟuḷ mey, aṭuttuvarum taṉimeyyuṭaṉō, aṭuttu varum uyirmeyyiluḷḷa meyyuṭaṉō mayaṅkum mayakkamē ciṟantamaiyiṉ, ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷāl atuvē viḷakkap paṭṭatu.
 e-ṭu:	pārttār - taṉimeyyoṭu taṉimey; yāttār - taṉimeyyōṭu uyirmey	(to.e.22 nac. urai)
oṉpatu, varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumāṟu -
oṉpatu iṉcāriyai peṟṟu varumoḻikaḷoṭu puṇarum. iṉcāriyai ‘iṟṟu’ eṉat tirintu puṇartalumuṇṭu.
oṉpatiṉ kalam, oṉpatiṟṟuk kalam, oṉpatiṉ cāṭi, oṉpatiṟṟuc cāṭi mutalāka aḷavuppeyarum, oṉpatiṉ kaḻañcu, oṉpatiṟṟuk kaḻañcu, oṉpatiṉ toṭi, oṉpatiṟṟut toṭi - mutalāka niṟaippeyarum, oṉpatiṉāyiram, oṉ patiṟṟukkōṭi - mutalāka eṇṇuppeyarum eṉa ivaṟ ṟoṭu nilaimoḻi ‘oṉpatu’ puṇarntavāṟu.	(to. e. 459, 470 nac.)
oṉpatiṉāḻi eṉpuḻi, varumoḻi nakaram varuvaḻi, iṉcāriyaiyiṉ ṉakaram keṭa, varumoḻi nakaram ṉakaramākat tirintu puṇarum. 	(459 nac.)
oṉpatu + pattu = toṇṇūṟu, oṉpatu + nūṟu = toḷ ḷāyiram; oṉpatu + āyiram = oṉpatiṉāyiram, oṉpatu + kaḻañcu, kalam = oṉpatiṉkaḻañcu, oṉpatiṉ kalam; oṉpatu + oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, kuruṇi = oṉpatiṟṟoṉṟu, oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭu, oṉpatiṟṟuk kuruṇi - eṉa varum.
oṉpatu+ pattu : varumoḻi pattu ‘nūṟu’ām;okarattoṭu takaramey poruntum; nilaimoḻiyil ‘patu’ keṭum; ṉakaram ṇakaramākat tiriyum.
oṉpatu + pattu ḻū oṉpatu + nūṟu ḻū toṉpatu + nūṟu ḻū toṇ + nūṟu = toṇṇūṟu.
oṉpatu +  nūṟu : varumoḻi nūṟu ‘āyiram’ ām. okarattoṭu takara meyporuntum; nilaimoḻiyil ‘patu’ keṭum : ṉakaram ḷakaramākat tiriyum. oṉpatu + nūṟu ḻū oṉpatu + āyiram ḻū toṉpatu + āyiram ḻū toṉ + āyiram ḻū toḷ + āyiram = toḷḷāyiram.
oṉpatu, eṇ niṟai aḷavuppeyarkaḷum piṟapeyarum varu moḻiyāka nikaḻumiṭattu, iṉ iṟṟu - eṉṟa cāriyaiyuḷ ēṟpatoṉṟu iṭaiyē varap puṇarum. eṭuttukkāṭṭu mēlē kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷamai kāṇka.	(naṉ. 194, 197)
oṉpatu vikāramum tiripumūṉṟil aṭaṅkutal -
virittal tōṉṟalākavum, valittalum melittalum nīṭṭa lum kuṟukkalum tiripākavum, tokuttalum mūviṭattuk kuṟaitalum keṭutalākavum aṭakki oṉpatu ceyyuḷ vikāramum tiripu mūṉṟeṉa amaiyum eṉpatu.	(i.vi. 58)
ceyyuḷiṭattu alvaḻi vēṟṟumaiyāl varum mūṉṟu vikāramum valittal mutaliya oṉpatu vikāramum varutalāl vēṟupāṭu aṟivataṟkum, virittal tōṉṟal vikāramākavum, valittalum melittalum nīṭṭalum kuṟukkalum tiritalāka vum, tokuttalum mūvaḻikkuṟaitalum keṭutalākavum, inta mūvakaiyuḷ avvoṉpatu vakai vikāraṅkaḷum aṭaṅkum eṉpatu aṟivittaṟkum, ceyyuṭkēyuriya oṉpatu vikāraṅ kaḷaiyum puṇarcci vikāraṅkaḷoṭu kūṟiṉār.	(naṉ. 156 irāmā.)
oṉṟaṉai oṉṟu paṟṟutal  -
itu vaṭamoḻiyil itarētarācrayam eṉṉum kuṟṟamām; anyōnyācrayam eṉavum peyar peṟum. itaṉait taṭumāṟṟam eṉavum kūṟupa.
iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum cārntu  ukaram kuṟukum eṉavum, iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum kuṟṟiyalukarattukkuc cārpāka varum eṉavum kūṟutal oṉṟaṉai oṉṟu paṟṟutalām.	(cū. vi. pak. 50)
viṉaimuṟṟuc col peyarāyavāṟṟāl ōcai vēṟupaṭum eṉavum, ōcai vēṟupaṭṭa kāraṇattāṉ viṉaimuṟṟuc col peyarām eṉavum kūṟutalum ‘oṉṟaṉai oṉṟu paṟṟutal’ eṉṟum kuṟṟamām.	(cū. vi. pak. 54)
kuṟṟiyalikaram eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭṭāl nāku+ yātu = nākiyātu - eṉṟu puṇarcciviti kūṟi, aṅṅaṉam ceykai ceyta piṉṉar avveḻuttaik kuṟṟiyalikaram eṉṟu kūṟuvatu ‘oṉṟaṉai oṉṟu paṟṟutal’ eṉṉum kuṟṟamām eṉiṉ, ākātu. nāku+yātu=nākiyātu - eṉṟu puṇarntavaḻi, appuṇarcci vaḻu eṉṟu kāṇaluṟṟuḻi, ‘yakaram varuvaḻi ikaram kuṟukum’ eṉṉumcūttiram vaḻānilai uṇartta vantatallātu, muṉ illāta kuṟṟiyalikarattai vitikka vantataṉṟu ātaliṉ, iṅṅaṉam kōṭaṟkaṇ ‘oṉṟaṉai oṉṟu paṟṟutal’ eṉṟa kuṟṟamillai.	(cū. vi. pak. 20, 21)
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷ nūṟu eṉṟa nilaimoḻiyoṭu 
puṇartal  -
nūṟu eṉpatu nūṟṟoṉṟu nūṟṟiraṇṭu, nūṟṟumūṉṟu, nūṟ ṟeṭṭu, nūṟṟuppattu, nūṟṟukkōṭi, nūṟṟut toṇṇūṟu, nūṟṟukkuṟai, nūṟṟitaḻt tāmarai, nūṟṟukkāṇam, nūṟṟuk kāl maṇṭapam- eṉa iṉaoṟṟu mikku, vaṉkaṇamāyiṉ varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum.	(to. e. 472. nac.)
nūṟṟorupaḵtu, nūṟṟirupaḵtu, nūṟṟeṇpatu - eṉa āṇṭum īṟṟiṉ iṉaoṟṟu iṭaiyē mikkup puṇarum. nūṟṟukkalam, nūṟṟukkaḻañcu - mutalāyiṉavum aṉṉa.	(473, 474 nac.)
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷ nūṟu eṉṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇartal  -
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷiṉ (ēḻ nīṅkalāka uḷḷavai) īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭum keṭṭu, mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki, oṉṟu iraṇṭu - eṉpaṉa oru iru - eṉṟāki, mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟu nakaramāka, nāṉkum aintum oṟṟunilai tiriyāvākap puṇarum.
	varumāṟu : oru nūṟu, iru nūṟu, munnūṟu, nāṉūṟu, ainnūṟu, aṟunūṟu, eṇṇūṟu.	(to. e. 460- 462 nac.)
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷ paḵtoṭu puṇarntu urupēṟṟal -
oṉṟu +paḵtu+ āṉ + ai
oṉṟu eṉpataṉoṭu pattu eṉpatu puṇarntu orupaḵtu eṉṟākum. ivvāṟē irupaḵtu muppaḵtu nāṟpaḵtu aimpaḵtu aṟupaḵtu (eḻupaḵtu) eṇpaḵtu - eṉpaṉavum ām.
orupaḵtu mutaliyaṉa urupukaḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi, paḵtu eṉpataṉkaṇ uḷḷa pakara oṟṟu nīṅkalāṉa ēṉaiya keṭa, āṉcāriyai peṟṟu orup+āṉ= orupāṉ- eṉṟāki, orupāṉai - orupāṉāl - orupāṟku- orupāṉiṉ - orupāṉatu - orupāṉkaṇ- eṉa urupēṟkum. iṅṅaṉamē irupāṉai, irupāṉāl.... mutalāyiṉavum koḷka.	(to. e. 199 nac.)

oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷ muṉ ‘pattu’ puṇartal  -
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ nilaimoḻiyāka niṟka, varumoḻiyākap pattu eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar varumiṭattu, varumoḻi iṭaiyoṟṟu nīṅkip ‘patu’ eṉavum aḵtu āytamākat tiriyap ‘paḵtu’ eṉavum amaintu puṇarum.
	varumāṟu :	oṉṟu + pattu = orupatu,  orupaḵtu
		eṭṭu  + pattu =  eṇpatu, eṇpaḵtu	(naṉ.195)
nilaimoḻi tirital ‘eṇṇuppeyarkaḷukkuc ciṟappuviti’  eṉpataṉkaṇ kāṇka.
oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟām eṇkaḷōṭu āyiram puṇarumāṟu -
orāyiram, ōrāyiram, irāyiram, īrāyiram : oru, iru - eṉpaṉa ukaram keṭṭum mutaleḻuttu uḷḷapaṭiyum nīṇṭum puṇarum.		(to. e. 464, 465 nac.)
mūvāyiram, muvvāyiram - mūṉṟaṉ ṉakaram vakaramāka, mutaleḻuttu uḷḷapaṭiyum kuṟukiyum puṇarum.	(466 nac.)
nālāyiram - nāṉkaṉ ṉakaram lakara oṟṟu ākum.	(467 nac.)
aiyāyiram - aintaṉ nakaram yakaramākum.	(468 nac.)
āṟāyiram, aṟāyiram - ukaram, varumoḻi ā ēṟi muṭintum, mutal ākāram kuṟukiyavaḻik keṭṭum puṇarum.	(469 nac.)
ēḻāyiram, eḻāyiram - ‘ēḻ’ iyalpākiyum neṭil kuṟuki yum puṇarum.	(392, 391 nac.)
eṇṇāyiram - eṭṭu ‘eṇ’ āki ṇakaraoṟṟu iraṭṭip puṇarum.	(444, 160 nac.)

oṉṟu mutalāṉa eṇkaḷ iraṭṭumāṟu -
oṉṟu mutal pattu īṟāṉa eṇkaḷuḷ oṉpatu oṉṟum nīṅkalāṉa oṉpatu eṇṇuppeyarkaḷum iraṭṭumāyiṉ (nilaimoḻi eṇṇuppeyarē varumoḻiyākavum nikaḻumāyiṉ), nilaimoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu nīṅkalākap piṟavellām keṭum. varumoḻi mutalil uyir varumāyiṉ vakarameyyum, meyvarumāyiṉ avvanta meyyum iṭaiyē mikappeṟum. (nilaimoḻi neṭil mutal kuṟukum eṉka.)
	varumāṟu : oṉṟu+ oṉṟu ḻū o + oṉṟu  ḻū o + v + oṉṟu = ovvoṉṟu; mūṉṟu + mūṉṟu  ḻū mu + mūṉṟu ḻū mu+m+mūṉṟu = mummūṉṟu. piṟavum ivvāṟē ivviraṇṭu, nannāṉku, aivaintu, avvāṟu, ev vēḻu, evveṭṭu, pappattu - eṉa muṭiyu māṟu koḷka.	(naṉ. 199)
oṉṟu muṉṉar uyirum yāvum vantu puṇarumāṟu -
varumoḻi mutalil uyirō, yā eṉṟa uyirmeyyō varu miṭattu, nilaimoḻiyākiya ‘oṉṟu’eṉpatu ‘oru’ eṉṟu tirinta nilaiyai nīkki ‘ōr’ eṉṟāki,
ōrakal, ōrāṭu, ōrilai, ōrī, ōrural, ōrūcal, ōrerutu, ōrēṇi, ōraiyam, ōroḻuṅku, ōrōṭam, ōrauviyam, ōryāṉai - eṉavarum. īraṭai, īryāṉai, mūvacai, mūyāṉai - eṉac ceyyuṭkaṇ varutalum koḷḷappaṭum.
(varumoḻi aḵṟiṇaiyāyavaḻiyē immuṭipu koḷka.)
oṉṟu mutaliya eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ aḵṟiṇaiyām. eṇṇiyaṟ peyarē uyartiṇaiyām. (to. e. 479 nac. urai)


ō
ō -
itu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil patiṉoṉṟām uyir; aṅkāttalōṭu itaḻ kuvittu eḻuppum uyirolikaḷuḷ oṉṟu; peyarākavum viṉaiyākavum varum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyām neṭil. ōkāra iṭaiccol pirinilai, viṉā, etirmaṟai, oḻiyicai, terinilai, ciṟappu - mutaliya pala poruḷil varum. 

ōo periyaṉ - eṉa uyarvuciṟappup paṟṟiyum, ō o koṭiyaṉ- eṉa iḻivuciṟappup paṟṟiyum, ōo tamakkōr uṟuti uṇarārē eṉak kaḻivirakkap poruḷ paṟṟiyum, ō perituvappak kēṭṭēṉ- eṉa makiḻccikkuṟippup paṟṟiyum, ōo koṭitu koṭitu - eṉa viyappuk kuṟippup paṟṟiyum, ō terintatu - eṉa ñāpakakkuṟippup paṟṟiyum, ō makaṉē - eṉa viḷikkuṟippup paṟṟiyum - ōkāram aḷapeṭuttum aḷapeṭātum mutaṟkaṇ varum.
ōkāra iṭaiccol puṇarumāṟu -
ōkāra iṭaiccol epporuḷil variṉum varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattōṭu iyalpākavē puṇarum.
‘ivaruḷ, yāṉō tēṟēṉ’(kuṟun.21) - pirinilai; naṉṟō tītō	 - terinilai; ōo koṇṭāṉ - ciṟappu; ‘kaḷiṟeṉkō koyyuḷaiya mā eṉkō’ - eṇ (puṟa.387); yāṉō koṇṭēṉ - māṟukōḷ eccam; nīyō koṇṭāy? - viṉā; puṟṟō putalō - aiyam; koḷalō koṇṭāṉ - oḻiyicai.		(to. e. 290, 291 nac. urai)
ōkāraīṟṟu iyalpu puṇarcci  -
ōkāra iṭaiccol moḻiyīṟṟil māṟukōḷ eccam, viṉā, aiyam, oḻiyicai - eṉṟa poruḷkaḷmēlum, teri nilai, eṇ, ciṟappu, īṟṟacai - eṉṟa poruḷkaḷmēlum varum. atu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu : yāṉō koṇṭēṉ.
kō eṉṟa peyar il eṉṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi, okaramākiya eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭai peṟātu (vakara) uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu iyalpākap puṇarum.
varumāṟu : kō+il ḻū kō+v+il = kōvil
	(to. e. 290, 291,293, nac.) (mu.vī. puṇar. 24)
ōkāraīṟṟuc cāriyai -
ōkāraīṟṟup peyar urupēṟkumiṭattum poruṭpuṇarccik - kaṇṇum oṉcāriyai peṟum. cāriyai peṟuṅkāl varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikātu.
e-ṭu : kō+ ai ḻū kō + oṉ + ai = kōoṉai 	(to.e.180 nac.)
		kō+kai ḻū kō+ oṉ + kai = kōoṉkai (294 nac.)
piṟkālattil oṉcāriyai ṉakarac cāriyaiyākavē, kōṉai, kōṉkai eṉṟu puṇarva āyiṉa.
ōkāraīṟṟuc col illoṭu kiḷattal  -
nilaimoḻi ōkāra īṟṟuc collāka, varumoḻi il eṉṟa col variṉ, ōkāra iṟutikkuriya oṉcāriyai peṟātu uṭampaṭumey aṭuttu iyalpākap puṇarum.
varumāṟu: kō+ il= kōvil (vakaram uṭampaṭumey) (to.e. 293 nac.)
‘kōmā muṉvariṉ yakaramum kutikkum’ (mu.vī. puṇariyal 24) eṉpataṉāl yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu, kō + il = kōyil - eṉṟātalē ikkālattup perumpāṉmai āyiṟṟu.
ōkāraīṟṟup potuppuṇarcci -
ōkāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum.	
e-ṭu  ōk kaṭitu, cōk kaṭitu (to. e.289 nac.)
ōkāra iṭaiccol epporuḷil variṉum, vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākavē puṇarum.
e-ṭu : yāṉō koṇṭēṉ (290, 291 nac.)
ōkāraīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭaiyākiya okaramum valleḻuttum peṟutalum, ṭu :	ōok kaṭumai, kōok kaṭumai, cōok kaṭumai;
	kōoṉkai, kōoṉcevi	urupupuṇarcci pōl oṉcāriyai peṟṟup puṇartalumuṇṭu.
	e-  (292, 294 nac.)
ōkāraīṟu oṉcāriyai peṟutal -
‘ōkāra īṟṟup potuppuṇarcci’ kāṇka.
ōcai iraṇṭāvaṉa -
eṭuttuk kūṟalum, paṭuttuk kūṟalum eṉa ōcai iruvakaip paṭum.	(mu.vī. eḻut. 39)
ōr aḷapu ākum aikāramum oḷakāramum -
moḻi iṭaiyilum kaṭaiyilum ai, ay - eṉpaṉa pōla olikkum aikāram orē māttirai aḷaviṟṟātalum aruki nikaḻvatu.
cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil (933) ‘ivaiyiṉ, kaviñar, cuvaiyiṉ, avaiyiṉ’ eṉa aṭiyetukaiyil aikāram ikaram pōla oru māttirai aḷavu olippataikkuṟippatām. āyiṉ, moḻi mutaṟkaṇ aikāram curuṅkātu.
piṟkālattil, moḻi mutal aikāram ‘ay’ pōlavum, oḷakāram ‘av’ pōlavum olittaliṉ, vīracōḻiyamum nēminātamum ai - ay eṉavum, oḷa - av eṉavum olikkum eṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl anta ai, oḷa - oṉṟarai māttirai olikkum. avai avvāṟu olittal moḻimutaṟkaṇṇām.
‘kauvai, vevvēl, avvēlē’; ‘koytakai, ceyyacan,  kaitaru, peytoḷi’ - eṉṟa aṭi mutaṟcīrkaḷai nōkki, oḷa- av eṉavum, ai- ay eṉavum olippataik kāṇalām.		(pu. ve. mā. 4 : 23; cīvaka. 1267)
tolkāppiyaṉārkku oḷakārakkuṟukkam uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu. 		(e. ā. pak. 63, 64)
ōr aḷapu icaittal  -
oru māttirai aḷaviṟṟāka olittal. a i u e o - eṉṟa kuṟṟuyir aintum, ivai meymītu porunta uṇṭākum uyirmeyk kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ toṇṇūṟum - ovvoṉ ṟum oru māttirai aḷaviṟṟāka olippatām.	(to.e. 3, 10 nac.)
aikārakkuṟukkam moḻi iṭaikaṭaikaḷil orumāttirai aḷaviṟku olippatu muṇṭu. kuṟṟukaram puṇarmoḻiyiṭai muṟṟiyalukaramāy orumāttirai aḷaviṟṟākum.	(57, 409 iḷa.)
oḷakārak kuṟukkamum oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyum orumāttirai aḷaviṉavāka olikkum eṉpatu naṉṉūl. (98)
ōr mayakka vakai -
makaraīṟāṉa aḵṟiṇaip peyarkaḷuḷ cila ṉakarattōṭu uṟaḻntu varum eṉpatu ōr mayakkavakai uṇarttalām. 
e-ṭu: nilam - nilaṉ.
moḻi mutalilum iṭaiyilum niṉṟa akara aikāraṅkaḷ tammil vēṟupāṭiṉṟic cakara ñakara yakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ variṉ okkum eṉpatum atu. e-ṭu : pacal - paical; mañcu - maiñcu; mayal - maiyal; aracu - araicu; ilañci - ilaiñci; arayar - araiyar.
aikāram yakaram eṉṟa ivaṟṟiṉvaḻiyē varum nakarattuṭaṉ ñakaram uṟaḻum eṉpārumuḷar.
	e-ṭu : ainnūṟu - aiññūṟu;  meyn naṉṟu - meyñ ñaṉṟu 	(naṉ. 121 - 123 mayilai.)
ōreḻuttoru moḻi -
ā ī ū ē ai ō ōḷa - eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ ēḻum eḻuttām taṉmaiyaṉṟic collām taṉmaiyum peṟum āṟṟala ātaliṉ,  ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷām.
ā - pacu; ī - koṭu;  ū- iṟaicci; ē- ampu; ō - matakunīr tāṅkum palakai; ai - viyappu; oḷa- avai (oḷakāram uyir meykkaṇallatu vārātu eṉpatē nac. karuttu)
iṉi, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ a i u - cuṭṭiṭaic coṟ kaḷākavum, ekaram viṉāiṭaiccollākavum, okaram oppiṭutalaik kuṟikkum pakutiyākavum variṉum ivai iṭaiccoṟkaḷātaliṉ, peyar viṉaikaḷāka varum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷai otta ciṟappila.	(to.e. 43, 44 nac.)
uyir neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷilum oḷakārattai vilakki ēṉaiya āṟaṉaiyum koḷvatē tolkāppiyaṉār karuttām. avar oḷakārattai ‘īraḷapu icaikkum iṟutiyil uyir’ eṉpar. 	(to.co. 281 cēṉā.)
uyir varukkattil āṟu, makara varukkattil āṟu, ta pa na - varukkaṅkaḷil aivaintu, ka va ca varukkaṅkaḷil nannāṉku, yakara varukkattil oṉṟu - ākiya neṭilkaḷ 40, no tu - eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷ iraṇṭu - āka nāṟpattiraṇṭum ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷām. ivai ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa. ciṟappillātaṉa piṟavumuḷa.
	varumāṟu : ā ī ū ē ai ō; mā mī mū mē mai mō; tā tī tū tē tai; pā pū pē pai pō; nā nī nē nai nō; kā kū kai kō; vā vī vai veḷa; cā cī cē cō;yā; no tu - eṉpaṉa.
uyirkkuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aintum, kuvvum, kau veḷa pī cai  eṉṉum uyirmeyneṭṭeḻuttukkaḷum ciṟappila. 	(naṉ. 129 irāmā.)
ivaṟṟiṉ poruḷ:
ā - pacu; ū- iṟaicci; ē-ampu; ai- viyappu;  ō- matakunīr tāṅkum palakai; mī, mē - mēl; mū- mūttal eṉpataṉ pakuti; mō - mōttal eṉpataṉ pakuti; tū - paṟṟukkōṭu; tē- teyvam, pā- pāṭṭu; pē- nurai; nē - aṉpu; nai- varuntu; nō- nōy; kū-pūmi; kō- aracu; vī- malar; vai- kūrmai; veḷa- kaikkoḷ; cī- nīkku; cē-erutu; cō- araṇ; yā- yāvai; no-tuṉpappaṭu; tu-uṇ. ivaiyāvum tamakkut tāmē mutalum iṟutiyum āvaṉa.	(naṉ. caṅ. 129)
tolkāppiyaṉār uyirmeyyai viṭuttu neṭil ēḻumē ōreḻuttorumoḻikaḷ eṉṟum, kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum moḻiyāka niṟaintu nillā eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. eṉavē, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ a i u - cuṭṭākavum e viṉāvākavum varum eṉṟa karuttuppaṭavum uyir varukkattiṟkē ōreḻut torumoḻi kūṟiyuḷḷār.	( to. e.  43, 44 nac.)
iṉi, ciṟappillaṉavum ōreḻuttorumoḻiyuḷ cila uḷavāyiṉa. avai āṟām uyirum, pakara īkāramum, ci cū cai kau veḷa- eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉavum koḷka.	(naṉ. 128 mayilai.)
vakaraīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarp poruḷai oppumaiyāṉ uṇartti niṟṟalāṉ oḷa eṉṉum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyum, cuṭṭu - viṉā- uvamaip poruḷait tarum iṭaiccol - ātalāṉ kuṟṟuyir aintāṉ ākiya ōreḻuttorumoḻi kaḷum,  kau- eṉṉum uyirmeyyāṉāya ōreḻuttoru moḻiyum, ivaipōlvaṉa piṟavum ciṟappillaṉa eṉak koḷka.	(naṉ. 129 caṅkara.)
oḷa
oḷa -
itu neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉuyirvaricaiyil paṉṉiraṇṭāva tākiya iṟuti yeḻuttām. iḵtu aṅkāttalōṭu itaḻ kuvitalāl  piṟakkum ainteḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. iḵtu aḷapeṭuppuḻi ukaram icainiṟaikka varum. iḵtu aḷa peṭukkuṅ kālattum aḷapeṭai iṉṟi varuṅkālattum poruḷ vēṟupaṭutaluṇṭu eṉat tolkāppiyam kūṟum. ‘au’ eṉpatu oḷakārattiṉ pōlivaṭivām; ‘av’ eṉpatum atu. oḷakāram moḻi mutaṟkaṇ kuṟukum eṉpar cilar. ‘auv’ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ kūṭṭam oḷakāram eṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar (cū. vi. pak. 25). uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ taṉittu iṟutiyil vārā eḻuttu iḵtu.
oḷakāraīṟṟup puṇarcci  -
oḷakāraīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukarappēṟum val leḻuttum, meṉkaṇam iṭaikkaṇam variṉum ukarap pēṟumeytip puṇarum. ciṟupāṉmai  urupu puṇarccikku vakutta iṉcāriyai peṟṟum vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcci nikaḻum.
e-ṭu : kauvuk kaṭitu, kauvuc ciṟitu, kauvut tītu, kauvup peritu; kauvu ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu; kauvu yātu, valitu, - eṉa al vaḻikkaṇṇum; kauvukkaṭumai, kauvucciṟumai, kauvuttīmai, kauvupperumai; kauvuñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci; kauvuyāppu, valimai; - eṉa vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ukaram peṟṟum, vaṉkaṇam variṉ valleḻuttum uṭaṉpeṟṟum puṇarnta vāṟu. (uyirkkaṇam variṉ ukaram peṟātu uṭampaṭu mey peṟṟē muṭital kauvaḻakitu, kauvaḻaku - eṉa iruvaḻiyum koḷka.)
iṉi, vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci iṉcāriyai peṟṟu, kauviṉ kaṭumai, kauviṉ ñāṟci, kauviṉ valimai, kauviṉarumai- eṉa muṭintavāṟum kāṇka.	(to. e. 295 nac.)
oḷakāraīṟu urupoṭu puṇartal  -
oḷakāraīṟṟuc col iṉcāriyai peṟṟu urupēṟkum. 
e-ṭu  	kauviṉai, kauviṉoṭu, kauviṟku.... kauviṉ kaṇ	(to. e. 173 nac.)
oḷakāraīṟu muṉṉilaiviṉaikkaṇ vārāmai -
oḷakāraīṟu muṉṉilai orumai ēvaṟkaṇ vārātu. aḵtu avvāṟu varuvatāyiṉ ukaraccāriyai peṟṟē varum; vaṉkaṇam variṉ uṟaḻntu muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : kauvu koṟṟā, veḷavu cāttā, veḷavu tēvā, veḷavu pūtā; kauvuk koṟṟā, veḷavuc cāttā....	(to. e. 152 nac.)
oḷakārak kuṟukkam -
oḷakārak kuṟukkam varumāṟu: oḷavai, pauvam - eṉa mutaṟkaṇ oḷakāram kuṟukiṟṟu. iṭaiyum īṟum vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. 
‘mummai iṭattum aioḷavum kuṟukum’ eṉa eṅkum oṭṭik koḷka. (aikāram moḻi mūviṭattum kuṟukumāṟu pōla, oḷakāramum moḻi mūviṭattum vantu kuṟukum eṉpatu nēmināta āciriyar karuttu. oḷakāram moḻi iṭaikaṭai kaḷil vārāmaiyiṉ avviṭaṅkaḷil oḷakārak kuṟukkam iṉṟu eṉpatu teḷivu.)	(nēmi. eḻut. 4 urai)
‘aikāra oḷakārak kuṟukkaṅkaḷ’ kāṇka.


oḷakāram aḷapeṭuttum aḷapeṭātum varutal  -
oḷaoḷa iṉic cālum - māṟupāṭu; oḷau iṉi vekuḷal - pōtumāṉatu eṉṟa kuṟippu; oḷau oruvaṉ iravalarkku īttavāṟu - viyappu (co. 281 cēṉā.)
kau - ‘niṉakkuk karuttāyiṉ kaikkoḷ’ eṉṟa aiyap poruḷ; kauu - ‘kaikkoṇṭēviṭu’ eṉṟa tuṇivup poruḷ; veḷa - ‘niṉakkuk karuttāyiṉ kaippaṟṟu’  eṉṟa  aiyapporuḷ; veḷau - ‘kaippaṟṟiyēviṭu’ eṉṟa tuṇivup poruḷ.	(co. 283 nac.)
ka
kaṭappāṭu aṟital  -
kaṭappāṭu - toṉṟutoṭṭu varum marapu. kaṭappāṭu aṟital - toṉṟutoṭṭu varum neṟimuṟaiyai aṟital.	(to. e. 37 nac.)
kaṭaikkuṟai -
oru pakāppatam īṟṟil kuṟaintuniṉṟu kuṟaiyāta colliṉ poruḷ tantu ceyyuḷil payaṉpaṭuvatu kaṭaikkuṟai eṉṉum vikāramām.
	e-ṭu : ‘nīluṇ tukilikai kaṭuppa’ (nīlam eṉpataṉ kaṭaik kuṟai ‘nīl’ eṉpatu)	(naṉ. 156)
kaṇkāl puṟam mutaliyaṉa  -
kaṇ, kāl, puṟam - mutaliyaṉa peyarāyum vēṟṟumaiyurupāyum niṟkum. urupāyavaḻi urupiṉ ceykaiyum peyarāyavaḻip peyariṉ ceykaiyum koṭukka.	(to. e. 202 nac. urai)
kaṇṇimai aḷavu -
iyalpāka orumuṟai iru kaṇkaḷum imaittaṟku nikaḻum kāla aḷavu. iḵtu eḻuttoli aḷavu kāṇpataṟku oru māttirai eṉṉum aḷaviṉaik kuṟippatām.	(to. e. 7 nac.)
kam: puṇarumāṟu -
kam eṉpatu kammiyaratu toḻil.  ittoḻilai uṇarttum kam eṉṟa col iruvaḻiyum, toḻiṟpeyar pōla, vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇamum iṭaikkaṇattu vakaramum variṉ ukaramum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : kammuk kaṭitu, kammu māṇṭatu, kammu valitu - al vaḻi; kammukkaṭumai, kammumāṭci, kammuvalimai - vēṟṟumai; yakaram varuvaḻi kam yātu, kam yāppu - eṉa iruvaḻiyum iyalpākap puṇarum; uyir varuvaḻi makaram iraṭṭittu kam maṭaintatu, kammaṭaivu - eṉap puṇarum.	(to.e.328 nac. urai) vēṟṟumaikkaṇ peyar varumoḻiyāka nikaḻiṉ, akkuc cāriyai iṭaiyē peṟṟuk kammakkuṭam, kammaneruppu, kammaviṟaku - eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum.	(329 nac. urai)
kamukan tōṭṭam
tōṭṭattuḷ piṟa maram ceṭikoṭikaḷ uḷavāyiṉum, mikuti paṟṟiya vaḻakkāl kamukantōṭṭam eṉappaṭumāṟu pōla, uyirīṟṟup puṇariyaluḷ puṇariyal paṟṟiya piṟa potuc ceytikaḷ iṭam peṟiṉum, perumpāṉmaiyum uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ varumoḻip puṇarcciyē kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, appeyar, mikuti paṟṟiya vaḻakkāl vantatu.	(naṉ. 151 caṅkara.)
kariyavai mutaliya coṟkaḷ urupēṟṟal  -
yāvai eṉṟa viṉāppeyar iṟuti aikāram vakarattoṭum keṭa vaṟṟuc cāriyai peṟṟu yāvaṟṟai - yāvaṟṟoṭu - eṉa muṭivatu pōla, aikāra īṟṟup paṇpukoḷ peyarkaḷākiya kariyavai - neṭiyavai - kuṟiyavai - mutaliyaṉavum īṟu mey yoṭum keṭa, vaṟṟuccāriyai iṭaiyē peṟṟuk kariya vaṟṟai - neṭiyavaṟṟai - kuṟiyavaṟṟai - eṉṟāṟ pōla urupoṭu puṇarum. ivai karumai - neṭumai - kuṟumai - mutaliya paṇpup peyaraṉṟik kariyavai - neṭiyavai - kuṟiyavai - mutalāṉa paṇpu koḷ peyarāka niṉṟu ‘vai’ keṭṭu ‘vaṟṟup’ peṟṟup puṇarntaṉa. (to.e. 178 nac. urai)
karuviyiṉ nāl vakaikaḷ -
akakkaruvi, akappuṟakkaruvi, puṟakkaruvi, puṟappuṟakkaruvi - eṉak karuvi nālvakaippaṭum. ivai muṟaiyē puṇarccikkuriya nilaimoḻi paṟṟi varumvitikaḷaik kūṟuvaṉa, puṇarcci yilakkaṇam- tiripu- puṇarccivakai- cāriyai- mutaliyaṉa paṟṟik kūṟuvaṉa, nilaimoḻi varumoḻikaḷāy niṟkum moḻikaḷaik kūṟuvaṉa, moḻikaḷ ātaṟkuriya eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum piṟappum kūṟuvaṉa ām.	(to.e. 1 nac. urai)
kaṉ:puṇarumāṟu -
kaṉ eṉpatu kaṉṉāruṭaiya toḻilaiyum, kaṉṉāṉaiyum, cempu eṉṟa ulōkattiṉaiyum  kuṟippatākum. aḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇamum iṭaikkaṇattu vakaramum variṉ ukaramum, yakaram variṉ iyalpum, uyirvariṉ ṉakaram iraṭṭutalum peṟṟu muṭiyum. 
	e-ṭu :	kaṉṉuk kaṭitu; kaṉṉu naṉṟu, kaṉṉu valitu; kaṉ yātu; kaṉ ṉaritu.
vēṟṟumaipporuṭpuṇarccikkaṇ alvaḻikkōtiya vitiyum peṟum; ukaraccāriyaiyai viṭuttu akkuccāriyai peṟutalu muṇṭu. 
	e-ṭu : kaṉṉukkaṭumai, kaṉṉakkaṭumai, kaṉṉakkuṭam, eṉavum; kaṉṉuñāṟci, kaṉṉañāṟci - eṉavum; kaṉṉuvaṉmai, kaṉṉavaṉmai - eṉavum varum. 	(to. e. 345, 346, nac. urai)
yakaram varuvaḻi iyalpākavum, uyir varuvaḻi ṉakaram iraṭṭittum puṇarum.  e-ṭu : kaṉyāppu, kaṉṉarumai
ciṟupāṉmai alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattu akaramum mel leḻuttum, meṉkaṇattu akaramum koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu : kaṉṉaṅ kaṭitu, kaṉṉa ñāṉṟatu, kaṉṉa māṇṭatu
		kaṉṉaṅkaṭumai eṉac ciṟupāṉmai kuṇa vēṟ ṟumaikkaṇṇum varum.	(345, 346 nac. urai)
miṉ - piṉ - paṉ- eṉum ṉakaraīṟṟuppeyar pōlavē, kaṉ eṉṉum nilaimoḻiyum, iruvaḻikkaṇṇum toḻiṟpeyar pōlavē ukaram peṟṟu muṭiyum. kaṉ eṉpatu vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci yāyiṉ, poruṭ peyaroṭu puṇaruṅ kāl akaram peṟṟu valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : kaṉṉuk kaṭitu, kaṉṉuc ciṟitu kaṉṉut tītu, kaṉṉup peritu; kaṉṉu ñāṉṟatu, kaṉṉu nīṇṭatu, kaṉṉu māṇṭatu; kaṉṉu valitu - alvaḻi
	kaṉṉukkaṭumai, kaṉṉucciṟumai, kaṉṉuttīmai,  kaṉṉupperumai; kaṉṉuñāṟci, kaṉṉunīṭci, kaṉṉu māṭci; kaṉṉuvalimai - vēṟṟumai
	kaṉṉakkuṭam, kaṉṉaccāṭi, kaṉṉattūtai, kaṉṉap pāṉai - vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci
iṉi, kuṇappeyaroṭu puṇaruṅkālattum, ciṟupāṉmai akaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭitalum, akaramum mel leḻuttum peṟṟu muṭitalum  koḷka. kaṉṉak kaṭumai, kaṉṉaṅkaṭumai - eṉavarum.	(i.vi.eḻut. 127)
kā eṉṉum niṟaippeyar puṇarumāṟu -
kā eṉṉum niṟaippeyar kuṟai eṉṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi, iṭaiyē iṉcāriyai peṟum. kāviṉ kuṟai - eṉavarum. iḵtu ummaittokai. ciṟupāṉmai kākkuṟai eṉaccāriyai peṟātu valleḻuttu mikutalumuṇṭu.		(to. e. 169 nac. urai)

kāriyakāla pakkam -
iḵtu atikārattiṉ iruvakaiyuḷ oṉṟu. aracaṉuṭaiya paṭaiyil tāmē ceṉṟu naṭattum taṇṭattalaivaraip pōla, ōriṭattu  niṉṟa col pala cūttiraṅkaḷoṭum ceṉṟu iyaintu taṉ poruḷaip payappippatu kāriyakāla pakkamām.
e-ṭu :	‘kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum’ (to.e.34 nac.) eṉṟa nuṟpāviluḷḷa ‘niṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟa coṟṟoṭar ‘kuṟṟiya lukaram vallāṟu ūrntē’ (36 nac.) eṉṟa nuṟpāt toṭaroṭu ceṉṟiyaintu poruḷ payappippatu pōlvaṉa. ēṉaiya vakai yatōttēca pakkamām.	(cū.vi.pak. 17)
kālappeyarp pakupatam -
kārāṉ, kārāḷ, kārār, kāratu, kāraṉa, kārēṉ, kārēm, kārāy, kārīr - eṉa ivvāṟu varuvaṉa, ikkālattai yuṭaiyār eṉṉum poruṇmaik kālappeyarp pakupata mām. (kār eṉṉum kālappeyar aṭiyāka ivai piṟantavai)	(naṉ. 133. mayilai.)
kālam kāṭṭum vikutikaḷ -
ṟu, ṟum - iraṇṭum iṟappum etirvum kāṭṭum.
	e.ṭu : ceṉṟu, ceṉṟum (ceṉṟēṉ, ceṉṟōm); cēṟu, cēṟum (celvēṉ, celvōm).
tu,tum - iraṇṭum avviru kālamum kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu : vantu, vantum (vantēṉ, vantōm); varutu, varutum (varuvēṉ, varuvōm)
ṭu, ṭum- iraṇṭumiṟappiṉaik kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇṭu, uṇṭum (uṇṭēṉ, uṇṭōm)
ku, kum- iraṇṭum etirviṉaik kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇku, uṇkum (uṇpēṉ, uṇpōm)
miṉ, ēvalviṉaikaḷiṉ īṟukaḷ, viyaṅkōḷ, i, mār - ivai  etirviṉaik kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇmiṉ, uṇ- tiṉ- cel- vā, uṇka, uṇṇuti, uṇmār (vantār)
pavvikuti iṟappum etirvum kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇpa
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu nikaḻvum etirvum kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	(kutirai) uṇṇum
etirmaṟai ākāravikuti mukkālamum kāṭṭum.
	e-ṭu :	uṇṇā	(naṉ. 145)
kiruttu
viṉaippakutimēl varum peyarvikuti kiruttu eṉappaṭum. 
	e-ṭu : naṭappatu, celpavaṉ - eṉpavaṟṟiluḷḷa tuvvikuti, aṉvikuti pōlvaṉa. 	(cū. vi. pak. 55)
‘kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirina’ -
eṭuttu ōtappaṭṭaṉa allāta coṟkaḷ ceyyuḷiṭattut tirintu muṭivaṉa eṉpatu iccoṟṟoṭariṉ poruḷ. avai varumāṟu:
akaraīṟṟu uriccol taṭavuttirai, taṭavunilai - eṉa varutal.
atavattaṅkaṉi - eṉa akaraīṟṟup peyar attup peṟutal.
naṟavaṅ kaṇṇi, kuravam nīṭiya - eṉa ākāraīṟṟup peyar akaramākak kuṟuki ammuccāriyai peṟutal.
muḻa, ira, cuṟa- eṉa ākāraīṟu kuṟuki ukaram peṟātu varutal.
kaḷḷiyaṅkāṭu - eṉa ikaraīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammup peṟutal.
‘tīyiṉ aṉṉa’ - eṉa īkāraīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iṉ peṟutal.
‘tiruvattavar’ - eṉa ukaraīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attup peṟutal.
‘ēp peṟṟa māṉ’- eṉa ēkāram vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ekarappēṟiṉṟi varutal.
‘kaittu uṇṭām pōḻtu’, ‘kaittu illavar’- eṉa aikāra īṟu attup peṟṟu varutal.
puṉṉaiyaṅkāṉal, mullaiyantoṭaiyal - eṉa aikāra īṟu ammup peṟutal.
kōyil - eṉa ōkāraīṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟutal.
akam+ cevi = añcevi eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ kakaraoṟṟum makaramum keṭutal.
maravam pāvai, marava nākam - eṉa iruvaḻiyum maram eṉpataṉ makaram keṭa ammup peṟutal. (marattāl āṉapāvai, nākamaram)
kāṉ- poṉ- eṉpaṉa, kāṉam - poṉṉantikiri - poṉṉaṅ kuvaṭu - eṉa ṉakaraīṟu iruvaḻiyum ammup peṟutal.
‘vetirattu naral icai’ - eṉa rakaraīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attup peṟutal.
‘nāvalan taṇpoḻil’- ‘kāṉalam peruntuṟai’- eṉa lakara īṟu vēṟṟumaiyil ammup peṟutal.
neytalañ ciṟupaṟai - eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ lakaraīṟu ammup peṟutal.
av + iṭai = āyiṭai - eṉa vakara oṟṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭital.
tev +muṉai = temmuṉai - eṉa vakaram keṭṭu makaram peṟṟu muṭital.
a eṉṉum cuṭṭu ‘aṉṟi’ eṉattirital.
kōṅkiṉ mukai, teṅkiṉ paḻam - eṉak kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu ‘iṉ’ peṟutal.	(to. e. 483. nac urai)
āraṅkaṇṇi - eṉa ār eṉṟa rakaraīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammup peṟṟu muṭintatu.
muḷavu mā, piṇavu nāy - eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ meṉkaṇam vantuḻi, ‘kuṟiyataṉ iṟuticciṉai’ keṭṭu ukaram peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
‘arumaruntaṉṉāṉ’ eṉaṟpālatu, arumaruntāṉ aru mantāṉ - eṉa marūuvāy muṭintatu.
cōṇāṭu - malāṭu - eṉpaṉavum cōḻaṉāṭu - malaiya-māṉāṭu -eṉpavaṟṟiṉ  marūumuṭipām. potuvil eṉaṟ pālatu potiyil eṉa muṭintatum atu.	(483 iḷa. urai)
kiḷaippeyarkaḷ puṇarumāṟu. -
kiḷaippeyarkaḷ, ṇakaram - ṉakaram- kuṟṟiyalukaram- eṉṟa mūṉṟu īṟṟaṉa. kiḷaippeyarāvaṉa ōriṉattai uṇarttum peyarkaḷ. uyartiṇaiyil ivai tiripiṉṟi varumoḻi yoṭu puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	umaṇkuṭi, cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi
		eyiṉkuṭi, cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi
		purōcukuṭi, cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi
pārppu + kuḻavi = pārppaṉak kuḻavi - eṉa aṉcāriyaiyum akkuc cāriyaiyum peṟṟatu. itu pārppiṉuḷ kuḻavi - eṉa vēṟṟumai muṭipiṟṟu.
aracu + akku+ kaṉṉi = aracakkaṉṉi, eṉa akkuccāriyai peṟṟatu.	(to. e. 307, 338, 418 nac.)
eyiṉakkaṉṉi eṉa ṉakaraīṟu akkup peṟutalum, veḷḷā ḷaṉ+ kumari = veḷḷāṇkumari - eṉavum vēḷāṇkumari eṉavum muṭital pōlvaṉavum koḷḷappaṭum.(338 nac. urai)
kīḻ:puṇarumāṟu -
kīḻ eṉṟa col varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpākavum mikkum puṇarum; iyalpē valiyuṭaittu.
e-ṭu :	kīḻ + kulam = kīḻkulam, kīḻkkulam (kīḻākiya kulam eṉap paṇputtokaiyātaliṉ alvaḻippuṇarcci eṉka.) (kiḻakku eṉṉum ticaippeyariṉ tiripākiya kīḻ vēṟu: kīḻmaippaṇpiṉaik kuṟikkum innilai- moḻi vēṟu.)	(naṉ. 226)
kīḻmai eṉṉum paṇpoṭu varumoḻi puṇartal pataviyaluḷ muṭittup pōntamaiyiṉ, ‘kīḻiruntum kīḻallār kīḻ allavar’ (kuṟaḷ 973) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kīḻ eṉpatu īṇṭup paṇpākupeyarām. ākavē, kīḻkulam kīḻkkulam, kīḻcāti kīḻccāti - eṉṉum ittoṭarkaḷ irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokaiyām eṉka.	(226 caṅkara.)
kīḻ eṉpatu nilaimoḻiyāka varumoḻi mutalil vaṉkaṇam varumāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum mikāmaiyum eṉṟairu nilaiyum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :kīḻkkuḷam, kīḻkuḷam; kīḻccēri, kīḻcēri; kīḻt tōṭṭam, kīḻtōṭṭam; kīḻppāṭi, kīḻpāṭi	(to. e. 395 nac.)
neṭumutal kuṟukātu ukaram peṟṟuk kīḻu kuḷam, kīḻucēri, kīḻu - tōṭṭam, kīḻupāṭi - eṉavum varum.	(396 iḷa. urai)

kuṇacanti -
akaraākāraṅkaḷ iṟutiyākiya moḻikku muṉṉar ikara īkāraṅkaḷ mutalākiya col variṉ, nilaimoḻiyiṟutiyum varumoḻimutalum keṭa, ōr ēkāram varum.
	e-ṭu : nara+intiraṉ = narēntiraṉ; umā+īcaṉ= umēcaṉ
akaraākāraṅkaḷ iṟutiyākiya moḻikku muṉṉar ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ mutalākiya col variṉ, nilaimoḻiyiṟuti yum varumoḻimutalum keṭa, ōr ōkāram varum.
e-ṭu  	tāma + utaraṉ = tāmōtaraṉ; kaṅkā+ uṟpatti = kaṅkōṟ patti. 	(mu. vī. moḻi. 39, 40)
akaraākāraṅkaḷil oṉṟaṉ muṉṉar ikaraīkāraṅkaḷil oṉṟu vantāl ēkāramum, avviraṇṭil oṉṟaṉ muṉ ukara ūkāraṅkaḷil oṉṟu vantāl ōkāramummuṟaiyē nilaippata īṟum varumpata mutalum keṭat tōṉṟutal kuṇacantiyākum.
	e-ṭu : cura+ intiraṉ = .curēntiraṉ, nara + intiraṉ = narēntiraṉ; tarā + intiraṉ = tarēntiraṉ, carva +  īcuraṉ = carvēcuraṉ; umā + īcaṉ = umēcaṉ, cita + intu= citēntu;		amala + uṟpavi = amalōṟpavi, makā + utaram = makōtaram; cutta + utakam = cuttōtakam, ñāṉa + ūrccitaṉ = ñāṉōrccitaṉ; mantira + ūki = mantirōki, tāma + utaraṉ = tāmōtaraṉ; tayā + uṟpatti = tayōṟpatti, tayā + ūrccitaṉ = tayōrccitaṉ.	(to.vi. 38 urai)
kuṇappeyarp pakupatam -
ceyyāṉ, ceyyāḷ, ceyyār, ceyyatu, ceyyaṉa, ceyyēṉ, ceyyēm, ceyyāy, ceyyīr, - eṉa ivvāṟu varuvaṉa ikkuṇattaiyuṭaiyār eṉṉum poruṇmaik kuṇappeyarp pakupatamām. (īṇṭuc cemmai eṉṟa kuṇappaṇpiṉ aṭiyāka ippeyarp pakupataṅkaḷ piṟantaṉa.)	(naṉ. 133 mayilai.)
kumiḻ eṉṟa marappeyar puṇarumāṟu -
kumiḻ eṉṟa marappeyar,pīr eṉṟa koṭiyiṉ peyar pōla, oruvaḻi melleḻuttum, oruvaḻi ammuccāriyaiyum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : kumiḻṅkōṭu, kumiḻaṅkōṭu	(to. e. 386 nac.)
kuyiṉ, ūṉ puṇarcci  -
ivviruvakaip peyarum iruvakaip puṇarccikkaṇṇum varumoḻi mutal vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum. kuyiṉ - mēkam: ūṉ - iṟaicci.
	e-ṭu :	kuyiṉ kaṭitu,ciṟitu, tītu, peritu - alvaḻi
		kuyiṉkaṭumai, ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai - vēṟṟumai
		ūṉ kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu peritu - alvaḻi
		ūṉkaṭumai, ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai - vēṟṟumai			(naṉ. 216)
kuyiṉ eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
kuyiṉ eṉpatu mēkattai uṇarttum col. atu vēṟṟumaik kaṇ vaṉkaṇam varumvaḻiyum tiriyātu iyalpāyp puṇarum
	e-ṭu : kuyiṉkuḻām, celavu, tōṟṟam, paṟaivu 											(to. e. 335 nac.)
kuṟṟiyalikaram (1) -
nāku yātu - eṉṟa toṭaril, ku oru māttirai peṟṟa muṟṟiyalukaram. atu keṭa, ataṉiṭattu ikaram vantu oli kuṉṟi araimāttirai aḷaviṟṟāya kuṟṟiyalikaram ākum. ākavē, kuṟṟiyalukaramē kuṟṟiyalikaramākat tiriyum eṉṟu naṉṉūl mutaliyaṉa kūṟum ceyti tolkāppiyaṉār karuttaṉṟu.
kuṟṟiyalikaram orumoḻikkaṇṇum toṭarmoḻik kaṇṇum varum. orumoḻikkaṇ muṉṉilai acaiccol lākiya miyā eṉpataṉ makarattai ūrntu varum ikaram kuṟṟiyalikaramām.
kuṟṟiyalukaram irumoḻikkaṇ iṭaiyē varumiṭattu muṟṟiyalukaramām. nilaimoḻiyīṟṟu ukaram keṭa, av viṭattu nāku + yātu = nākiyātu eṉak kuṟṟiyalikaram varum.		(to. e. 409, 411 iḷa.)
kuṟṟiyalikaram ceyyuḷil mey pōla alaku peṟātu; mey aḷapeṭuttal pōl aḷapeṭukkātu. kuṟṟiyalikarattukkum muṟṟiyalikarattukkum iṭaiyē poruḷ vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. avvēṟupāṭṭaik kaṟpōr eḷitāka uṇaravēṇṭik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṉ mēl puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭupa.
nāṭi)yātu, kokki)yātu, kuṟumpi)yātu - kuṟṟiyalikaram
nāṭi yātu, kokki yātu, kuṟumpi yātu - muṟṟiyalikaram		(e. ā. pak. 89) (cey. 4 nac. urai)
kuṟṟiyalikaram (2)  -
orumāttirai peṟṟa ikaram taṉ māttirai kuṟaintu araiyāka olippatu kuṟṟiyalikaramām. varumoḻi mutalil yakaram vara, nilaimoḻi īṟṟil niṟkum kuṟṟiyalukaram ikaramākat tiriyum. av vikaram kuṟṟiyalikaramām. miyā eṉṉum muṉṉilai acaiccolliṉ makarattai ūrntu niṉṟa ikaramum taṉ māttiraiyil kuṟaintolikkum kuṟṟiyalikaramām. īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram 36 ātaliṉ av vakaiyāl varuvaṉavum, acaiccol miyāviṉkaṇ varuvatum eṉa ivvāṟu kuṟṟiyalikaram 37 āmāṟu kāṇka.	(naṉ. 93)
kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram cārpeḻuttātal  -
cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉāl pirappaṅkōl ākātu cantaṉak kōlē āmāṟu pōla, uyiriṉ kuṟukkamum uyirēyām eṉiṉum, puṇarcci vēṟṟumaiyāṉum, poruḷ vēṟṟumai yāṉum, cīrum taḷaiyum citaiyumiṭattu alaku peṟāmai yāṉum, kuṟṟiyalikara kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ cārpeḻuttu eṉa uyiriṉ vēṟāyiṉa.	(i.vi. 16)
kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram : peyarkkāraṇam  -
kēṇmiyā, varaku eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ ikaramum ukaramum, ‘pōlum’ eṉpuḻi araimāttiraiyāy niṉṟa makaram ‘pōṉm’ eṉa lakaram ṉakaramāyt tirintu īroṟṟuṭaṉ niṉṟa kāraṇattāl piṉṉark kālmāttiraiyāy niṉṟāṟpōla, muṉṉar orumāttiraiyāy niṉṟu piṉṉar oru kāraṇam paṟṟi araimāttirai ākāmal, makaramum valleḻuttumākiya meyyiṉaic cārntu eṉṟum araimāttiraiyāyē niṟkum oru taṉmaiya āyiṉum, appeṟṟi uṇarātu, ivai arai māttirai peṟutal makarakkuṟukkam pōlac ceyaṟkaiyāl pōlum eṉṟu ulakam malaiyāmaip poruṭṭu, ivai iṅṅaṉam ātal iyalpu eṉpār, kuṟṟikaram kuṟṟukaram eṉṟoḻiyātu, kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟu kuṟiyiṭṭār.
‘ikaram kuṟukum’, (to. e. 410) ‘ukaram  kuṟukiṭaṉ’ (e. 406) eṉṟa toṭarkaḷukku, ikaram aṇukum (vantu poruntum), ukaram vantu poruntum iṭaṉ - eṉpaṉavē poruḷ.	(cū. vi. pak. 27)
kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇariyal nilaiyiṭaik kuṟukal  -
kuṟṟiyalikaram coṟkaḷ puṇarntiyalum nilaimaikkaṇ taṉakkuriya arai māttiraiyiṉum kuṟuki olittaṟkum uriyatu; kuṟuki oliyāmaiyē perumpāṉmai eṉka.
e-ṭu :	āṭu+yātu= āṭiyātu; kavaṭu + yātu = kavaṭi yātu; toṇṭu+  yātu = toṇṭiyātu - īṇṭuk  kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟuki olittaṉa.
	teḷku + yātu = teḷkiyātu; nāku+ yātu = nākiyātu - īṇṭuk kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟukātu arai māttiraiyē olittaṉa.
muṉṉavai kuṟukum eṉṟaṟkum piṉṉavai kuṟukā eṉṟaṟkum kāraṇam varumāṟu: āṭi (tiṅkaḷ), kavaṭi (veḷvaraku), toṇṭi (ūr) eṉṉum (muṟṟiyal) ikara īṟṟup peyarkaḷ yātu eṉṉum varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, āṭiyātu - kavaṭiyātu - toṇṭiyātu - eṉa varum. ātaliṉ, āṭu+yātu = āṭiyātu. kavaṭu + yātu= kavaṭiyātu, toṇṭu + yātu = toṇṭiyātu - eṉa varum ivvirutiṟattiṟkum vēṟṟumai pulappaṭa vēṇṭi, kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭa āṇṭut tōṉṟum āṭi - kavaṭi - toṇṭi - eṉṉum kuṟṟiyalikarattiṉ māttirai araiyiṉum kuṟukik kālāka olikka vēṇṭuva tāyiṟṟu. tiṅkaṭ peyarākiya āṭi mutaliyavaṟṟatu iyalpu ikara īṟṟiṉai oru māttiraiyaḷaviṟku icaittal vēṇṭum.
iṉit teḷkiyātu - nākiyātu - eṉpavai kuṟukāmaikkuk kāraṇam, teḷki - nāki - eṉpaṉa iyaṟpeyarkaḷ ākāmai yāl nilaimoḻi teḷku - nāku - eṉa aiyamiṉṟi uṇarap paṭum ātaliṉ, avai arai māttiraiyākavē kuṟaiyātu olikkum eṉṟavāṟu.	(to.e.35. ca. pāla.)
kuṟṟiyalikaram ‘puṇariyal nilaiyiṭaik kuṟukalum urit’tātal -
kuṟṟiyalikaram taṉimoḻikkaṇṇē yaṉṟi, nilaimoḻi varumoḻiyoṭu puṇartal iyaṉṟa nilaimaiyākiya toṭar moḻikkaṇṇum vantu poruntutal uṭaiyatām. iṅṅaṉam varuvatu irumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram ām.
kuṟukutal - aṇukutal, vantu poruntutal.
kuṟṟiyalikaram āytam pōla vēṟeḻuttāvatām; aṉṟi, oru māttirai peṟṟu niṉṟa ikaram, makarakkuṟukkam  pōla, oru kāraṇam paṟṟi araimāttiraiyāyk kuṟaintu niṉṟataṉṟu.		(cū. vi. pak. 27, 28)

kuṟṟiyalikaram mutaliyaṉa tamiḻcciṟappeḻuttu ātal -
iṅku (peruṅkāyam), ētu tātu eṉpaṉa kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu pōla iruppiṉum, avai vaṭacollātaliṉ muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉavē, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉa alla eṉka. eṉavē, kuṟṟiya lukaram tirintu kuṟṟiyalikaram ātalum vaṭamoḻikkaṇ illai. āytam tamiḻcciṟappeḻuttu eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. ākavē, kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum vaṭamoḻikkaṇ kāṇappaṭāta, tamiḻiṟkē uriya ciṟappeḻut tukkaḷām.	(cū. vi. pak. 28)
kuṟṟiyalikara muṟṟiyalikaram poruḷ vēṟupāṭu -
nilaimoḻi kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟatāka varumoḻi mutalil yakaram variṉ, ukaram keṭak kuṟṟiyalikaram peṟṟup puṇarum.
iyalpāṉa muṟṟiyalikarac col muṉṉarum yakaram vantu puṇarum.
kokku nāṭu kuṟumpu eṉpaṉa mutalāṉa kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuccoṟkaḷum, kokki nāṭi kuṟumpi eṉpaṉa mutalāṉa muṟṟiyalikara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷum, kokkiyātu - nāṭiyātu -kuṟumpiyātu eṉṟē puṇarum. nilaimoḻiyīṟu ki ṭi pi eṉak kuṟṟiyalikaram ākiyavaḻi, nilaimoḻikaḷ  kokku nāṭu  kuṟumpu  eṉpaṉa; ki ṭi pi muṟṟiyalikaram ākiyavaḻi nilai moḻikaḷ kokki nāṭi kuṟumpi eṉpaṉa. (kuṟumpu pālainilattūr; kuṟumpi - puṟṟam paḻañcōṟu)
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu alvaḻippuṇarcci  -
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷellām perumpālum avvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāka muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu : nāṭu kaṭitu, varaku kaṭitu, teḷku kaṭitu, eḵku kaṭitu, kuraṅku kaṭitu - eṉa valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi nīṅkalāka ēṉaiyavai iyalpākap puṇarum.
valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi kokkuk kaṭitu eṉa varumoḻi mutalil varum valleḻuttu iṭaiyil mikkup puṇarum.
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum viṉaikkuṟippuc collum iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	kiṭantatu kutirai, karitu kutirai
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu nilaimoḻi vaṉkaṇam vara irupeya roṭṭup paṇputtokaipaṭap puṇarumiṭattu, iṉaoṟṟu mikku valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭitalum, iyalpukaṇam vara avvāṟu puṇarumiṭattu iṉaoṟṟu mikutalum uṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	karaṭu + kāṉam = karaṭṭukkāṉam; kuruṭu + kōḻi = kuruṭṭuk kōḻi; karaṭu + vaḻi = karaṭṭu vaḻi; kuruṭu + maṉitaṉ = kuruṭṭu maṉitaṉ.
ciṟupāṉmai aṉcāriyaiyum akkuccāriyaiyum peṟutalu muṇṭu. e-ṭu : pārppu + kuḻavi ḻū pārppu + aṉ + akku + kuḻavi = pārppaṉakkuḻavi
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷuḷ, melloṟṟu valloṟ ṟāy aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum uḷa; melloṟṟut tiriyātu aikāramum valloṟṟum peṟuvaṉavum uḷa.
	e-ṭu : ōryāṇṭu + kuḻavi = ōryāṭṭaik kuḻavi	paṇṭu + cāṉṟōr = paṇṭaic cāṉṟōr
iyalpukaṇam vantavaḻi, melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāram māttiram peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum uḷa.
	e-ṭu : ōryāṇṭu + yāṉai = ōryāṭṭai yāṉai	aiyāṇṭu + erutu = aiyāṭṭai yerutu
viṉaiyeccat toṭarkkaṇ vaṉtoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukara īṟu vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikkup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	cettuk kiṭantāṉ, naṭṭup pōṉāṉ
meṉtoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉaiyeccam iyalpāka varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	iruntu koṇṭāṉ, vantu pōyiṉāṉ
ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷ uṇara niṉṟa iṭaic coṟkaḷākiya āṅku īṅku ūṅku yāṅku yāṇṭu āṇṭu īṇṭu - mutaliyaṉavum, aṅku iṅku uṅku eṅku - mutaliyaṉa vum, meṉtoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷā yiṉum vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi mikkup puṇarum.
āṅkucceṉṟāṉ.... yāṅkucceṉṟāṉ.... īṇṭuc ceṉṟāṉ.... aṅkuc ceṉṟāṉ..... mutalākap puṇartal kāṇka.
āṇṭu eṉpatu iṭaiccollāyiṉ, āṇṭuc ceṉṟāṉ eṉa valleḻuttu mikum. āḷ eṉṟa pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟanta iṟantakālac ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉaiyecca māyiṉ, āṇṭu koṇṭāṉ eṉa iyalpāka muṭiyum.	(to. e. 425 - 429 nac).
uṇṭu eṉpatu uṇmaittaṉmaip paṇpiṉai uṇarttiya-vaḻi, nāṟkaṇamum variṉum uṇṭu poruḷ, ñāṉam, yāḻ, āṭai eṉa iyalpāyum, pakaram varumoḻimutalil varuvaḻi,
uṇṭu poruḷ, uḷporuḷ eṉa irumuṭivum peṟṟum puṇarum.
uḷporuḷ eṉa ōcai piḷavupaṭātu kūṟiṉ paṇput tokai yām; ōcai iṭaiyaṟavupaṭac colliṉ (‘uṇṭu poruḷ’ eṉpatu poruḷātaliṉ) kuṟippu viṉaimuṟṟut toṭarām.	(nac. urai).


kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟup puṇarcciyiṉ ciṟappuviti -
avvaḻippuṇarccikkaṇ, vaṉtoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟiṉ muṉ varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikum; ēṉait toṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟukaḷ iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : pākkuk kaṭitu; nāṭu ciṟitu, eḵku tītu, varaku peritu, vantu pōṉāṉ, eytu poruḷ.
vēṟṟumaip puṇarccikkaṇ, iṭaittoṭar - āytat toṭar - iṭaiyil oṟṟu mikap peṟāta neṭil toṭar -iṭaiyil oṟṟu mikāta uyirttoṭar - eṉṉum ikkuṟṟiya lukara īṟukaḷ vaṉkaṇam variṉ mikā.
	e-ṭu : mārpu kaṭumai, eḵku ciṟumai, nāku tīmai, aracu perumai 
ṭu ṟu - iṟutiyākiya neṭiltoṭarum uyirttoṭarum nilai moḻiyāka niṟpa, varumoḻimutal vaṉkaṇam variṉ, nilaimoḻi ṭ ṟ eṉpaṉa iṭaiyil mika, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum; piṟakaṇam variṉum iraṭṭutal koḷka.
	e-ṭu : yāṭṭuk kāl, pāṟṟuc ciṟai; muraṭṭu maṉitaṉ, vayiṟṟiṭai
ippuṇarcci perumpāṉmaiyum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇatu. ‘muraṭṭu maṉitaṉ’: alvaḻi muṭipu.
cila meṉtoṭark kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ vēṟṟumaip puṇarcci yil vaṉtoṭark kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ ākum.
	e-ṭu : maruntu + pai = maruttuppai; kaṉṟu + ā = kaṟṟā
cila meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ puṇarccikkaṇ aikāra īṟṟaṉavām. vaṉtoṭarāyt tirintu īṟṟu aikāram peṟuvaṉavumuḷa.
	e-ṭu : paṇṭu + kālam = paṇṭaikkālam; iṉṟu + nāḷ = iṟṟaināḷ.
ticaippeyarkaḷ īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram, piṟaticaippeyarum ēṉaiya peyarum varumoḻiyāka nikaḻumiṭattu, tāṉ ēṟiya meyyoṭu keṭa, īṟṟayal kakara oṟṟuk keṭṭum, ṟakaram ṉakaramākavum lakaramākavum tirintum, piṟavāṟum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : vaṭakku + kiḻakku, mēṟku, cēri = vaṭakiḻakku, vaṭa mēṟku, vaṭacēri; teṟku + kiḻakku, mēṟku, cēri = teṉkiḻakku, teṉmēṟku, teṉcēri; mēṟku + kāṟṟu, ūr = mēlkāṟṟu, mēlūr; kiḻakku + kāṟṟu, cēri = kīḻkāṟṟu, kīḻaiccēri; mēṟku + cēri = mēlaiccēri.
teṅku eṉṟa nilaimoḻimuṉ ‘kāy’ variṉ, nilaimoḻi mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu īṟṟuyirmey keṭṭut tēṅkāy eṉa muṭiyum. (iṭaiyē amcāriyai peṟṟut teṅkaṅkāy eṉa muṭitalumuṇṭu).	(naṉ. 181 - 187)
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu marappeyarp puṇarcci -
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟaṉavākiya (akakkāḻaṉavākiya) marappeyar kaḷum (puṟakkāḻaṉavākiya) puṟpeyarkaḷum ammuccāriyai peṟṟu vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarum.	e-ṭu	:	tēkku + am + kōṭu, cetiḷ tōl, pū = tēkkaṅ kōṭu, tēkkañ cetiḷ, tēkkantōl, tēkkam pū; kamuku + am + kāy = kamukaṅkāy; cīḻku + am + pul = cīḻkampul; kampu + am + pulam = kampam pulam; payaṟu + am + kāy = payaṟṟaṅkāy.(to. e. 415 nac).
marappeyarkaḷuḷ īṟṟayal eḻuttākiya melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tiriyāta marappeyarkaḷum, tiriyum marap peyarkaḷum uḷa.
	e-ṭu :	puṉku + am + kōṭu = puṉkaṅkōṭu;kuruntu + am + kōṭu = kuruntaṅkōṭu - melloṟṟu val loṟṟu ākātaṉa.
		vēmpu + am + kōṭu = vēppaṅkōṭu; kaṭampu + am + kāy = kaṭappaṅkāy; īñcu + am + kulai = īccaṅkulai - ivai melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tirintaṉa.	(416 nac.)
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu muṉṉilai viṉaikkaṇ vārāmai  -
muṉṉilai ēvalorumai viṉaikkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu vārātu. vārāteṉavē, muṉṉilai ēvalorumaikkaṇ varum ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu īṟukaḷ muṟṟiyalukaramākavē olikkap paṭum. e-ṭu : muṟukku, paracu, kaṭṭu, kattu, eḻuppu, tīṟṟu 	(to. e. 152 nac. urai)
kuṟṟiyalukara eṇṇikkai -
taṉineṭil ēḻuṭaṉē, āytam oṉṟum, moḻi iṭaiyīṟu kaḷil varappeṟāta oḷakāram oḻittu oḻinta uyir patiṉoṉṟum, valleḻuttu āṟum, melleḻuttu āṟum, valleḻuttoṭu toṭarāta vakaram oḻittu oḻinta iṭaiyeḻuttu aintum ākiya muppattāṟu eḻuttukkaḷuḷ, yātāṉum oṉṟu īṟṟukku ayaleḻuttākat toṭarappaṭṭu, moḻiyiṟutikkaṇ valleḻuttukkaḷuḷ yātāṉum oṉṟu paṟṟukkōṭāka ataṉai ūrntu varum ukaram taṉ arai māttiraiyiṉ kuṟukum. (īṟṟayal eḻuttākiya iṭavakai yāṉ kuṟṟiyalukaram 36 āyiṟṟu)	(naṉ. 94 civañā.)
kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal -
itu tolkāppiya eḻuttuppaṭalattiṉ oṉpatāvatākiya iṟuti iyal. ukaram kuṟuki varum iṭaṅkaḷ, īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ, toṭarmoḻiyil varum ukaram, palavakaik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumai - alvaḻi - eṉṟa iru nilaiyilum nikaḻum puṇarccikaḷ, cuṭṭuppeyar viṉāppeyar niṟaippeyar aḷavuppeyar ivaṟṟiṉ puṇarccikaḷ, virivāṉa muṟaiyil eṇṇuppeyarp puṇarcci, puḷḷimayaṅkiyalil oḻintu niṉṟa ceyyuḷmuṭipukaḷ, puṇariyalnilaiyiṭaip pakuttuk kāṇātu uḷḷapaṭiyē koḷḷattakka toṭarkaḷ, eḻuttatikārap puṟaṉaṭai - eṉpaṉa kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariya luḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.	(to. e. 406 - 483 nac.)
kuṟṟiyalukarap potuppuṇarcci -
nilaimoḻiyīṟṟiṉkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟka varumoḻi mutalil uyir varumāyiṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ēṟiniṉṟa meyyai viṭuttut tāṉ keṭum; ciṟupāṉmai keṭātu niṉṟu uṭampaṭumey peṟṟup puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	nāku + aritu ḻū nāk + aritu = nākaritu; aḻaippatu + ē ḻū aḻaippatu + v + ē = aḻaippatuvē (naṉ. 303); ātu + um ḻū ātu + v + um = ātuvum (naṉ. 300)	(naṉ. 164)
kuṟṟiyalukaram (1) -
itu tolkāppiyam kuṟippiṭum cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu. itu taṉakkeṉat taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyil piṟappiṭa miṉṟit tāṉ ēṟiniṟkum meyyiṉ piṟappiṭamē taṉakkup piṟappiṭamākak kōṭaliṉ, orē eḻuttākiya kuṟṟiyalukaram piṟappiṭam nōkki āṟākak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. itu tamiḻilum malaiyāḷattilumē uḷḷatu. kuṟṟiyalukaram ēḻu toṭarkkaṇ varum eṉavum, ataṉāl nāṟpattiraṇṭu ākum eṉavum, āṟu toṭarkkaṇ varum eṉavum, ataṉāl muppattāṟu ākum eṉavum kūṟuvaṉa ēṟpuṭaiyaṉa alla. toṭarnōkkik kuṟṟiyalukarattai āṟākak kōṭalē amaiyum. aḵtu īreḻuttorumoḻi, uyirttoṭar moḻi, iṭaiyoṟṟut toṭarmoḻi, āytattoṭar moḻi, valloṟṟuttoṭarmoḻi, melloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi eṉa eṭuttukkūṟi viti kūṟutaṟkup payaṉpaṭu kiṟatu. moḻimutaṟkaṇ varum kuṟṟiyalukarattaiyuṭaiya col kiḷaippeyarākiya nuntai eṉpatu. im moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram oṉṟē. itu nuntai eṉṟa peyarkkaṇaṉṟi, viṉaikkaṇ vārātu. ikkuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷi peṟuta liṉṟu.
taṉimoḻi īṟṟiluḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷi peṟutaliṉ atu puḷḷiyīṟu ākum. ataṟku māttirai arai.
alvaḻi vēṟṟumaip poruṇmaikkaṇ varum toṭar- moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram irumoḻikku iṭaiyē varuvaḻi, atu muṟṟiyalukaramām. āyiṉ valloṟṟu iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram varumoḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu varuvaḻit taṉ paḻaiya arai māttirai aḷaviṟṟākavē, cekkukkaṇai - cukkukkoṭu mutaliya coṟṟoṭarkaḷil niṟpatumuṇṭu.
valloṟṟuttoṭarmoḻi īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram val leḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyākiyavaḻik kāl māttirai peṟum eṉavum, ēṉaiya alvaḻi vēṟṟumaip puṇarccikkaṇ araimāttirai peṟum eṉavum kūṟuvatu tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukku ēlātu. paṉmoḻip puṇarcci yākiya ceyyuḷil muṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalukaram iraṇṭaṉaik koṇṭum nērpu niraipu acaikaḷai amaittuk koṇ ṭamai, kuṟṟiyalukaram toṭarmoḻikkaṇ orumāttirai peṟum iyaipai nōkkiyē ām.
peyarccoṟkaḷuḷ araimāttirai peṟum kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuccoṟkaḷ accollē ēvalviṉaiyāyiṉ avai orumāttirai peṟum muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc collām. perukku - kaṭṭu - kātu - eṉpaṉa peyarāyiṉ, kuṟṟukara īṟāy araimāttirai peṟum īṟṟeḻuttuṭaiyaṉavām. kuṟṟiya lukara īṟu ēval viṉaikkaṇ vārātu eṉpatu tolkāppi yaṉār karuttām.
kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟu pōlat taṉmītu varumoḻimutal uyir ēṟa iṭam koṭukkum. muṟṟiyalukaraīṟu keṭum eṉṟu kūṟum tolkāppiyaṉār ōriṭattum kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭum eṉṟu kuṟippiṭavillai. eṉavē, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟu pōla araimāttirai koḷvataṉoṭum puḷḷi peṟuvata ṉoṭum amaiyātu, meyyīṟṟiṉ ceykai peṟṟup puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ aṭakkappaṭṭatu.
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟil avvukaram keṭa niṉṟa meyyīṟṟiṉ mītu uyir ēṟi muṭiyum eṉṟu kūṟuvatum cālātu. moḻiyiṟuti yil vārāta k c ṭ t p ṟ eṉṟa valliṉameykaḷ āṟaṉaiyum īṟṟil koḷḷa uṭaṉ iṇaittuk koḷḷappaṭṭa eḻuttē kuṟṟiyalukaram. meyyiṉ tāṉattil vantuḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ nilaiyum meyyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ nilaiyē. kuṟṟiyalukaram meykkuriya araimāttirai aḷavē olitta liṉ, āṇṭu ukaraoli ammeyyeḻuttiṉait terivikkum tuṇaiyē niṉṟatu. olittal eḷimai karutiyē, ēḻ uṇ tiṉ vav maṇ pal eṇ mutaliya coṟkaḷ ēḻu uṇṇu tiṉṉu vavvu maṇṇu pallu eṇṇu mutaliya coṟkaḷākat tiritalai iṉṟum kāṇkiṟōm.
kuṟṟiyalukaram toṭarmoḻikkaṇ orumāttirai peṟṟu muṟṟiyalukaram pōla niṟṟaliṉāltāṉ, ceyyuḷil acaik kuriya eḻuttukkaḷ uyir - uyirmey - kuṟṟiyalukaram - eṉa mūṉṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ceyyuḷāyiṉum cīriṭai aṭutta colloṭu cērātu ōcai iṭaiyaṟavupaṭṭavaḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram araimāttirai aḷaviṟṟē yāki alaku peṟāta nilaiyaik kāṇkiṟōm. itu piṟkāla nilai.
‘kuṉṟu kōṭu nīṭu’ - mūvacaiccīr
cīr īṟṟil varum kuṟṟukaram muṟṟukaramāki alaku peṟutalai yum kāṇkiṟōm.
‘pāṟṟiṉam ārppap paruntu vaḻippaṭara’ - paruntu: muṟṟukara īṟu. kuṟṟiyalukarattait tolkāppiyaṉār uyirīṟṟil aṭakkavillai. īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷi peṟutaliṉ atu meyyīṟu pōlak koḷḷappaṭṭu uyirēṟa iṭam koṭukkum eṉpatē tol. karuttu.
naṉṉūlār kuṟṟiyalukarattai uyirīṟṟuḷ aṭakki akkuṟṟiya lukaram uyirvariṉ keṭum eṉṟār. 	(vē. reṭṭiyār āyvukkaruttu)
kuṟṟiyalukaram (2) -
taṉineṭil, āytam, (meyyiṉai ūrnta) uyir, valliṉa mey, melliṉam, iṭaiyiṉamey eṉṉum ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu īṟṟayal eḻuttāka vara, colliṉ iṟutiyil valliṉap puḷḷiyai ūrntu varum ukaram, taṉ oru māttiraiyiṉ kuṟaintu araimāttiraiyāka olittaliṉ kuṟṟiyalukaramām. īṟṟayaleḻuttai nōkkik kuṟṟiyalukaram neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram - āytat toṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram - uyirt toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram - vaṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram, meṉ toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram, iṭaittoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉa āṟu vakaippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟiya lukaram oṉṟē īreḻuttoru moḻiyāka varum; ēṉaiya ellām pala eḻuttukkaḷālākiya moḻiyāka varum.
kuṟṟiyalukaram āṟu valliṉa meykaḷaiyum paṟṟukkōṭākak koṇṭu varutaliṉ, āṟu vakaiyum āṟu īṟṟeḻuttoṭum uṟaḻak kuṟṟiyalukaram 36 eṉpar.
	e-ṭu : nāku, kaḵcu, kavaṭu, pattu, ceṉṟu, mārpu eṉa āṟu toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷaiyum muṟaiyē kāṇka.	(naṉ. 94)
kuṟṟiyalukaram iṭavēṟṟumai paṟṟi aṟuvakaippaṭum eṉpatu -
neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻu, āytam oṉṟu, iṭaiyiṟutikaḷil vārāta oḷakāram nīṅkalāka uyir patiṉoṉṟu, valleḻuttu āṟu, melleḻuttu āṟu, valleḻuttukkaḷoṭu toṭa rāta vakaram nīṅkalāka iṭaiyeḻuttu aintu - ākiya ivaṟṟai aṭuttu varum kuṟṟiyalukaram, īṟṟayaleḻuttai nōkkik kaṇakkiṭa (7+1+11+6+6+5) muppattāṟām; iṭattai nōkka, neṭil - āytam - uyir - vali - meli - iṭai - eṉṉum ivaṟṟai aṭuttu varutaliṉ aṟuvakaippaṭum.	(naṉ. 94 civañā).
kuṟṟiyalukaram iṭavēṟṟumai paṟṟi 42 ātal  -
taṉineṭil, kuṟiliṇai, kuṟilneṭil, neṭiloṟṟu, kuṟiliṇai oṟṟu, kuṟilneṭiloṟṟu, kuṟṟoṟṟu eṉṟa ēḻu acaikaḷaiyum aṭuttu k c ṭ t p ṟ eṉṟa āṟu mey kaḷaiyum ūrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram varavē, ataṉ eṇṇikkai (7 ō 6 =) 42 ākum eṉpa. iṅṅaṉam vakutta varaiyaṟaiyil, piṇṇākku - cuṇṇāmpu - paṭṭāṅku - viḷaiyāṭṭu - iṟumpūtu mutaliyaṉa aṭaṅkā. avaṟṟuḷḷē, ṇākku - ṇāmpu - ṭāṅku - yāṭṭu eṉpaṉavaṟṟai neṭiloṟṟiṟuti eṉavum, pūtu eṉpataṉai neṭiliṟuti eṉavum koṇṭu, ivaṟṟiṉkaṇ varum kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟu koḷḷiṉum, pōvatu - varuvatu - oṉpatu - eṉpatu - mutaliyaṉa ippakutiyil aṭaṅkā. ātaliṉ kuṟṟiyalukarattai ataṉmuṉ niṟkum acaikaḷ paṟṟi 42 eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭal cālātu.(naṉ. 94 caṅkara.)
kuṟṟiyalukaram iṭavēṟṟumai paṟṟi 36 eṉal -
īṟṟayal eḻuttaic ciṟappākak koṇṭu, neṭil 7-āytam - oḷa nīṅkalāṉa uyir 11 - valliṉam 6 - melliṉam 6 - vakaram nīṅkalāṉa iṭaiyiṉam 5 āka 36 eṉṟu tokai koḷvar. immuṟaiyil ekara okaraṅkaḷoṭu vallukaram cērntuvarum colliṉmaiyāl, avaiyiraṇṭaiyum nīkkak kuṟṟiya lukaram 34 ākum. ātaliṉ immuṟai eṇṇikkaiyum cālātu.
taṉineṭil, āytam, uyir, vali, meli, iṭai eṉṟa aṟuvakaippaṭṭa ivaṟṟait toṭarntu ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu eṉṟa āṟu kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷum varutaliṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram 36 eṉpa. iṭaiyeḻuttukkaḷ āṟaṉuḷ enta eḻuttaiyum ṭu ṟu eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ toṭarāmaiyiṉ, ivaṟṟai nīkkak kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattu nāṉkē ātaliṉ, immuṟaiyil kuṟṟiyalukarattaik kaṇakkiṭṭu 36 eṉalum cālātu.
kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ūrntu varum valliṉamey paṟṟiyō, taṉmuṉ uḷḷa eḻuttiṉ iṉam paṟṟiyō āṟu eṉṟa kaṇakkiṭutalē poruttamāṉatām. kuṟṟiyalukaram eḻuttu vēṟṟumaiyāl āṟu; iṭavēṟṟumaiyāl āṟu eṉa iv viru tiṟaṉaiyum uṟaḻntu kūṟāmal taṉittaṉiyākak kūṟutalē takkatu.	(e. ā. pak. 163).


kuṟṟiyalukaram orōvaḻi uṭampaṭumey peṟutal  -
‘taṉmuka mākat tāṉaḻaip patuvē’ (naṉ. 303) - itil ‘aḻaippatu’ kuṟṟukaramoḻi; ataṉōṭu ēkāra iṭaic col vantu ‘aḻaippatē’ eṉa muṭiya vēṇṭiyatu vakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu ‘aḻaippatuvē’ eṉa niṉṟatu. ‘āṟaṉ orumaikku atuvum ātuvum’ (naṉ. 300) - itaṉkaṇ ‘ātu’ kuṟṟukaramoḻi; ataṉōṭu um eṉṉum iṭaiccol vantu ‘ātum’ eṉ muṭiya vēṇṭiyatu, vakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu ‘ātuvum’ eṉa niṉṟatu.	(naṉ. 164 irāmā.)
kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭutal  -
‘uyirvariṉ ukkuṟaḷ meyviṭ ṭōṭum’ eṉa naṉṉūlār nūṟpā amaittamai pōlat tolkāppiyaṉār potuviti amaittilar. avar nūṟu eṉpataṉ muṉṉum āṟu eṉpataṉ muṉṉum muṟaiyē oṉṟu mutaliya eṇkaḷum āyiramum vantu puṇariṉ, nilaimoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭa, niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl uyir vantu oṉṟi muṭiyum eṉṟār. (nūṟu+ oṉṟu = nūṟṟoṉṟu; āṟu + āyiram = āṟāyiram) oṉṟiṉam muṭittal eṉpataṉāl ēṉaiya kuṟṟiyalukaramum uyirmutal moḻi variṉ keṭa, niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl varumoḻi mutal uyir vantu oṉṟi muṭiyum eṉpatu peṟṟām.
kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉmītu uyirēṟi muṭiyum eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamē muṟṟiyalukarattiṉ mītum uyirēṟi muṭiyalām. avvāṟē oruvarum kūṟilar. kuṟṟiyalukarattukkum uyir eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭu koṇṭamaiyiṉ, oṟṟiṉmēlaṉṟi uyiriṉmēl uyirēṟi muṭital poruntātu. nākaritu - eṉpuḻi, itaḻ ciṟitu kuvitalākiya muyaṟci peṟappaṭāmaiyiṉ, kuṟṟiyalukara ōcai ittoṭarkkaṇ illai. yakaram varuvaḻi ikaram ukarattiṉ mītu ēṟi muṭiyum eṉṉātu, ukaram keṭa, ikaram kuṟukik kuṟṟiyalikaramāki varum eṉpatē āciriyarkaruttu. eṉavē, kuṟṟiyalukaram uyir varuvaḻik keṭumē aṉṟi uyirēṟa iṭam tārātu.	(cū. vi. pak. 40)
nūṟu muṉ mūṉṟu, nāṉku eṉpaṉa puṇarumvaḻik kuṟṟiya lukaram keṭṭatāyiṉ, nūṟṟ mūṉṟu - nūṟṟ nāṉku - eṉṟē attoṭar amaiyum. āṟu mutal kuṟuki ‘aṟu’ eṉṟu muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟatu āyiṉamaiyiṉ, avvukaram keṭṭatu. kuṟṟiyalukarattukku uyir eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭṭait tol. vaḻaṅkavillai. toṭarmoḻiyil kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiya lukaram eṉṟa tol. karuttai yuṭkoṇṭāl, kuṟṟiya lukaram keṭum eṉṟu karutaṟku iṭaṉ ēṟpaṭātu - eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa karuttukkaḷāl civañāṉamuṉivar karuttu maṟukkap paṭukiṟatu.	(to. e. 472, 469 nac.) (e. ā. pak. 176, 177)
kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarum muṟai -
īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟu pōlap puḷḷi peṟum. atuvum puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkum. kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭamkoṭukkum eṉpatē iḷampūraṇar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, i.vi. āciriyar, e. ā. āciriyar ākiyōr karuttām. naṉṉūlār uyir variṉ nilaimoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟiya lukaram keṭum eṉṟār. kuṟṟiyalukaramum muṟṟiyalukaram pōlak keṭum eṉpatē civañāṉamuṉivar karuttām.
nāku + aritu = nākaritu. nākaritu eṉpuḻi, muṉṉark kuṟṟukara ōcaiyum piṉṉar uyirōcaiyum peṟṟu, avviraṇṭum kūṭi niṉṟallatu apporuḷ uṇarttal ākāmaiyiṉ, uyirēṟuṅkāl kuṟṟukaram keṭṭup pōka niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl uyir ēṟiṟṟu eṉṟal poruntātu.	(i.vi. eḻut. 65 urai)
kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷi peṟutal -
īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukarattiṉ māttiraiyil tāṉ pātiyē peṟuvatu eṉpataik kuṟippiṭa, meyyīṟu pōlap puḷḷiyiṭṭu eḻutappeṟum eṉpar pērāciriyar, mayilai nātar, caṅkaranamaccivāyar, civañāṉamuṉivar, e.ku. āciriyar, e.ā. āciriyar mutaliyōr. puḷḷi peṟātu eṉṉum karuttiṉar iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum. naṉṉūlār itupaṟṟi oṉṟum kūṟavillai.
moḻi mutalil kuṟṟukaram varum nuntai eṉṟa colli luḷḷa nu - puḷḷi peṟātu.	(e.ku. pak. 110)
‘kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa’ (to. e. 105 nac.) eṉṟu nūṟpāvil, kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiyata ṉāl, moḻimutal kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatu tolkāppiyaṉārkku uṭaṉpāṭākalām.
kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟu -
kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟu eṉṟatu eṉṉeṉiṉ, orumatam kuṟainta āṉaimukakkaṭavuḷai ‘mummatattaṉ’ eṉṟāṟpōla, ilakkaṇai eṉṉum viti paṟṟik kūṟiṉār eṉka. neṭil ēḻum, āytam oṉṟum, moḻi iṭaiyilum iṟutiyilum vārāta oḷakāram nīṅkiya uyir patiṉoṉṟum, valloṟṟu āṟum, melloṟṟu āṟum, valleḻuttoṭu toṭarāta vakaraoṟṟu nīṅkiya iṭaiyoṟṟu aintum āka muppattāṟum īṟṟayal eḻuttāka vara, avaṟṟai nōkkak kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟu eṉpa.	(naṉ. 94 irāmā.)
‘āṉaimukattāṉai mummatattaṉ eṉal’ kāṇka.
kuṟṟiyalukaram meḻimutaṟkaṇ varal -
nuntai eṉṟa muṟaippeyariṭattu varum ‘nu’, itaḻaic ciṟitu kuvitta aḷavil tōṉṟum oliyāy, moḻimutaṟkaṇ varum kuṟṟiyalukara māyiṟṟu. itaṉai muṟṟiyalukaramāka olippiṉum poruḷvēṟupā ṭiṉṟu.	(to. e. 67, 68 nac.)
‘kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟu’ -
kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkum.		(to. e. 106 iḷa., 105 nac. urai)
kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟu pōla īṟṟil varuvaḻip puḷḷi peṟum.	(pērā., cū. vi., e. ku., e.ā.)
kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟu pōlap puḷḷi peṟum. puḷḷi peṟutal māttirai cempātiyātalaik kuṟippatākalā ṉum, kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷipeṟutal ‘muppāṟ puḷḷi’ eṉṟa toṭarālēyē pulaṉāvatākalāṉum, moḻimutaṟkaṇ varum kuṟṟukaram puḷḷi peṟātu eṉpatu āciriyar karuttākātu.
kuṟṟiyalukara muṟṟiyalukarap puṇarcci -
kuṟṟiyalukaram araimāttirai aḷaviṟṟu; varivaṭivil puḷḷi peṟuvatu. eṉavē īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟu pōlap puḷḷiyīṟākum. puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉmītu varumoḻimutalil varum uyirēṟi muṭiyum. ākavē, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭam koṭuppatu. kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭātu; muṟṟiya lukaramāyiṉ ukaram keṭa niṉṟa meyyiṉmītu uyirēṟi muṭiyum.
“aṟu eṉṟa muṟṟukarattiṟkē īṇṭu kēṭu kūṟiṉār; eṉṉai? kuṟṟukaramāyiṉ ēṟimuṭitaliṉ”	(to. e. 469.nac. urai)
“aḵtu + aṉ + ai - āytam keṭāmuṉpē aṉṉiṉ akarattaik kuṟṟukarattiṉ mēl ēṟṟuka; āytam keṭṭāl atu muṟṟukaramāy muṭitaliṉ.”	(422 nac. urai)
ittolkāppiya eḻuttatikāra nac. uraippakutikaḷ uḷaṅkoḷat takkaṉa.
kuṟṟiyalukara muṟṟiyalukarap poruḷ vēṟupāṭu -
perukku, kaṭṭu mutalāyiṉa peyarākiyavaḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷām. avai muṉṉilai ēvalorumai viṉaiyākiyavaḻi muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷām. kātu, kattu, murukku, teruṭṭu mutalāyiṉavum aṉṉa.	(to. e. 68 nac. urai)
kuṟṟiyalukara muṟaippeyar -
nuntai eṉṟa muṟaippeyaril mutaleḻuttākiya nakarattai ūrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram vantuḷḷatu. itaṉai itaḻaic ciṟitu kuvittuk kuṟṟiyalukaramāka olippiṉum, mikutiyum kuvittu muṟṟiyalukaramāka olippiṉum poruḷ vēṟupāṭu illai.		(to. e. 68 nac. urai)
kuṟṟukaraīṟṟut terinilai viṉaimuṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷ -
naṭa vā - mutalāka aḵku īṟākak kūṟappaṭṭa ivvirupattu mūṉṟum uyirum oṟṟum kuṟṟukaramum ākiya īṟṟavākip paṭuttalōcaiyāṉ acceykaimēl peyarttaṉmaip paṭṭu viṉaimāttiraiyē uṇartti niṟpaṉa. (viṉaippakuti kaḷākiya mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyariṉ iyalpuṭaiya ivaṟṟai vaṭanūlār ‘tātu’ eṉpa). kuṟṟukarattai vēṟu pirittataṉāl, pōkku - pāyccu - ūṭṭu - naṭattu - eḻuppu - tīṟṟu - it toṭakkattu vāypāṭṭāṉ varuvaṉavum koḷka.	(i.vi. 43 urai)
kuṟṟukara īṟṟu nāṟperunticaikaḷ -
avai vaṭakku,teṟku, kuṇakku, kuṭakku eṉpaṉa.		(naṉ.185 mayilai.)
kuṟṟukaram, kuṟṟikaram iraṇṭiṉmēlum puḷḷiyiṭutaliṉ payaṉ -
tātu - ētu - eṉṟal toṭakkattu āriya moḻikaḷum, eṭṭu - koṭṭu - eṉṟal toṭakkattup potumoḻikaḷum, kuṉṟi yātu - nāṭiyātu - eṭṭiyāṇṭuḷatu - eṉṟal toṭakkattup puṇarmoḻip poruḷ vēṟupāṭukaḷum aṟitaṟporuṭṭuk kuṟṟukaram kuṟṟikaraṅkaḷukku mēl puḷḷi koṭuppārumuḷar.		(naṉ. 97 mayilai.)
āriyamoḻikaḷil īṟṟukkuṟṟukaram nikaḻātu. eḷ + tu, koḷ+tu - ivai eṭṭu koṭṭu eṉa vanta coṟkaḷ ēval viṉaiyātaliṉ āṇṭuk kuṟṟukaram vārātu. eṭṭu - koṭṭu - eṉpaṉa puṇarmoḻi yākātu taṉiccollāy niṉṟavaḻi viṉaic collākāmal peyarāyiṉaviṭattē kuṟṟukaramām. kuṉṟi - nāṭi - eṭṭi - eṉpaṉa nilaimoḻiyākātu, kuṉṟu- nāṭu - eṭṭu 
eṉpaṉavē nilaimoḻiyāy. varumoḻi mutaṟ kaṇ yakaram varutaliṉ īṟṟu ukaram tirinta ikaramē kuṟṟiya likaramām.
kuṟṟukara vāypāṭṭu viṉaippakutikaḷ -
kuṟṟukarattai vēṟu pirittu ōtiya ataṉāṉ, pōkku pāyccu uruṭṭu kaṭattu eḻuppu tīṟṟu - eṉṟal toṭakkattu vāypāṭṭāṉ varuvaṉavum, allā īṟukaḷāṉ varum vāypāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉavum ‘cey’ ēvalil aṭaṅkum eṉṟu koḷka.	(naṉ. 136 mayilai.)
kuṟṟeḻut taḷapeṭai -
kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷāy nīṇṭu mīṇṭum oli mika aḷapeṭai eḻuttup peṟum nilai kuṟṟeḻuttaḷa peṭaiyām.
varum eṉpatu, ‘varūum iṟuti’ (to. co. 9) ‘yāteṉa varūum’ (co. 32) eṉṟāṟ pōla, varūum eṉak kuṟṟeḻut taḷapeṭai āyiṟṟu.
	e-ṭu	:	eḻu	-	‘eḻūut tāṅkiya katavu’	(puṟanā. 97)
			kuḻu	-	‘kuḻūuk kaḷiṟṟuk kuṟumpu’	(puṟanā. 97)
			paḻu	-	‘paḻūuppal aṉṉa’	(kuṟun. 180)
			paru	-	‘parūuppiṇiya toṭi’	(puṟanā. 97)
ivvāṟu kuṟṟeḻuttuḷḷaviṭattu aḷapeṭai vantatu.		(to. e. 261 nac. urai)
‘kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum moḻi niṟai’ yāmai -
kuṟṟeḻuttu (uyir) aintāvaṉa a i u e o eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mutal mūṉṟum cuṭṭu iṭaiccol; ekaram viṉāiṭaiccol; okaram ‘oppu’ eṉṟa paṇpiṉ pakuti yāyt taṉittu vārātu eṉpa.
ivai peyarō viṉaiyō ākāmal iṭaiccollākap peyarccollaic cārntu niṉṟē cuṭṭupporuḷum viṉāpporuḷum uṇarttutalāṉum, okaram oppuppaṇpiṉ pakuti eṉpatu ellārkkum uṭaṉpāṭu aṉmaiyāṉum, ivai moḻiniramputal illai eṉpatu. ‘aintum moḻi niṟaipila’ eṉavē, nāṉku iṭaiccollāy moḻi niṟaikkalām eṉpatum karuttām.	(to. e. 44 nac. urai)
kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ iṭainiṉṟa oṟṟai māttirai mikuttal -
kuṟṟeḻuttup palavāka varutalāṉ tōṉṟum vaṇṇam kuṟuñcīrvaṇṇam. akkuṟṟeḻuttup payiṉṟu varutalāṉ, iṭainiṉṟa oṟṟiṉ oli mikum.
 e-ṭu : ‘kuraṅṅkuḷaip polinta koycuval puravi’  (akanā. 4)
eṉa muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉṟa kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷiṉ ōcaiyāṉ ṅakara oṟṟoli nīṇṭavāṟu. (kuraṅṅkuḷai)	(to. e. 50 nac. urai)
kuṟṟeḻuttu  vēṟupeyarkaḷ -
kuṟumai eṉiṉum, iraccuvam eṉiṉum, kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉiṉum oruporuṭkiḷavi.	(mu.vī. eḻut. 9)
kuṟṟoṟṟāka vārā meykaḷ -
rakaraoṟṟum ḻakaraoṟṟum taṉikkuṟiliṉpiṉ oṟṟāka vārā. avai neṭil, kuṟṟiliṇai ivaṟṟiṉ piṉṉarē oṟṟāka varum; kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyākavē varum.
	e-ṭu :	tār, avar, vara, karu; tāḻ, itaḻ, uḻa, maḻu					(to. e. 49 nac. urai)
kuṟaḷ maḵkāṉ -
makarakkuṟukkam. lakara ḷakara meykaḷ tirinta ṉakara ṇakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ varum makaram, taṉ araimāttiraiyiṟ kuṟukik kāl māttiraiyāy oru moḻi iṟutikkaṇ varum. nilaimoḻi makaraīṟṟu viṉaiyāka niṟka, varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ vakaram nikaḻumāyiṉ, nilaimoḻiyīṟṟu makaram kuṟukik kāl māttiraiyāy irumoḻikkaṇ varum. ivvāṟu iṭa vakaiyāl makarak kuṟukkam mūṉṟām.
	varumāṟu : pōlum ḻū pōlm ḻū pōṉm, maruḷum ḻū maruḷm ḻū maruṇm - orumoḻi makarakkuṟukkam	
tarum vaḷavaṉ - irumoḻi makarakkuṟukkam.	(naṉ. 96)
kuṟiccūttiram -
palavaṟṟaiyum kuṟittu aṟiya varum cūttiram kuṟiccūttiram. ‘ammutal īrāṟu āvi’ eṉavum, ‘valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa’ eṉavum, aṟital aḷavāy varum cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟic cūttiraṅkaḷ.	(naṉ. 20 irāmā.)
kuṟi, ceykai viḷakkam -
cūttiravakai āṟaṉuḷ ivai cila. kuṟiyāvaṉa ivai uyir, ivai oṟṟu, ivai peyar, ivai viṉai eṉṟal toṭakkattu aṟitalmāttiraiyāy varuvaṉa. kuṟi eṉpatu aṟitalai uṇarttiya mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyar. ceykai yāvaṉa patam muṉ vikutiyum patamum urupum puṇarum puṇarcci viti aṟintu, aṅṅaṉam aṟital māttiraiyāy nillātu avvāṟu vēṇṭuḻip puṇarttalaic ceytalum, peyarum viṉaiyum koḷḷum muṭipu viti aṟintu aṅṅaṉam aṟital māttiraiyāy nillātu avvāṟu vēṇṭuḻi muṭittalaic ceytalum mutaliyaṉa.	(naṉ. 20 caṅkara.)
‘kuṟittu varu kiḷavi’ -
nilaimoḻip poruḷ niramputaṟku ataṉaik kuṟittu varum collākiya varumoḻi.
	e-ṭu : cāttaṉ vantāṉ - eṉṟa toṭaril, cāttaṉ eṉpatu nilai moḻiyākiya niṟutta col; vantāṉ eṉpatu varumoḻi yākiya kuṟittu varu kiḷavi. 	(to. e. 107 nac.)

kuṟippicai -
‘āviyum oṟṟum aḷaviṟantu olikkum iṭaṅkaḷ’ kāṇka.
kuṟiyataṉ kīḻ ā -
taṉikkuṟilai aṭutta ākāraīṟṟup peyar kuṟukutalum, ataṉōṭu ukaram ēṟṟalum, iyalpākavē ākāraīṟāy varutalum eṉṟa mūṉṟu nilaiyum ceyyuṭku uriya.
e-ṭu : nilā, nila, nilavu; palā, pala, palavu; 	cuṟā, cuṟa, cuṟavu; kaṉā, kaṉa, kaṉavu
‘nilā vaṇaṅku’ eṉavum, ‘nila viri kāṉal’ eṉavum, ‘eṉ ceyyumō nilavu’ eṉavum ceyyuṭkaṇ varumāṟu kāṇka.		(naṉ. 172)
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉ uru iraṭṭal -
taṉikkuṟṟeḻuttai aṭuttuc colliṉ iṟutiyil varum oṟṟukkaḷ varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ uyirvariṉ iraṭṭum. iṅṅaṉam oṟṟu iraṭṭuvaṉa ñakāra ṅakāra rakāra ḻakāram oḻintaṉa. īṟṟil varum meykaḷ ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṟa patiṉoṉṟām. ivaṟṟuḷ ñakāram uriñ eṉṟa orē moḻikkaṇṇum, nakāram porun - verin - eṉṟa iru moḻikkaṇṇum aṉṟi vārā. ivai kuṟṟoṟṟu alla. ra ḻa eṉpaṉa kuṟṟoṟṟāka vārā. eṉavē, ṇa ma ṉa ya la va ḷa eṉṟa ēḻu meykaḷumē taṉikkuṟilai aṭuttu moḻi yīṟāy variṉ iraṭṭum.
	e-ṭu : maṇ + aritu = maṇṇaritu; kam + aritu = kammaritu; poṉ + akal = poṉṉakal; mey + arumai = meyyarumai; kal + aritu = kallaritu; tev + aritu = tevvaritu; kaḷ + arumai = kaḷḷarumai.
iraṭṭutal iruvaḻikkaṇṇum ām.	(to. e. 160 nac. urai)
kuṟiyatu -
kuṟṟeḻuttu; kuṟil eṉavumpaṭum. ippeyarāṉ, uyirkkuṟ ṟeḻuttum, uyirmeykkuṟṟeḻuttum amaiyum. itaṉ māttirai oṉṟu.	(to. e. 160 nac).
kuṟil -
a i u e o eṉṟa aintum oru māttiraiyē peṟum uyirkkuṟṟeḻuttām. ivai 18 meykaḷmītu ēṟavē amaiyum (18 ō 5 =) 90 eḻuttukkaḷum uyirmeyk kuṟṟeḻuttām. kuṟilukku māttirai oṉṟu.	(naṉ. 64, 87)
kuṟiliṇai, kuṟilneṭil muṉṉar oṟṟumey keṭutal -
kuṟil māttirai nīṇṭu neṭilākum. ākavē, kuṟil neṭil eṉpaṉa māttiraikoṇṭu vevvēṟu peyar peṟṟaṉa. irukuṟil iṇaintu iraṇṭu māttirai koṇṭaṉavum, kuṟil neṭil iṇaintu mūṉṟu māttirai koṇṭaṉavum, iraṇṭu māttirai aḷaviṟṟāya neṭilpōla, varu moḻik kaṇ t n variṉ, nilaimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa l ḷ - eṉṟa meykaḷ keṭum.  (varumoḻi mutaliluḷḷa takaram ṟakaramāka vum ṭakaramākavum tiriyum; nakaram ṉakara ṇakaraṅkaḷākat tiriyum.)
e-ṭu :	kōḷ + tītu = kōṭītu; viral + tītu = viraṟītu; varāl + tītu = varāṟītu; kōḷ + nimirntatu = kōṇi mirntatu; viral + nimirntatu = viraṉimirntatu; varāl + nimirntatu = varāṉimirntatu; 
tol. neṭilmuṉ oṟṟuk keṭaṟkē viti kūṟi, kuṟiliṇai muṉṉarum kuṟilneṭil muṉṉarum oṟṟuk keṭaṟku viti kūṟātu, pōnta poruḷāl  koḷvittār.	(to. e. 50 iḷa. urai)
kuṟukkal vikāram -
ceyyuḷ vikāram āṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu. ceyyuṭkaṇ taḷai citaiyāmai vēṇṭi neṭṭeḻuttuk kuṟukuvatu kuṟukkal vikāramām. e-ṭu : ‘tiruttārnaṉ ṟeṉṟēṉ tiyēṉ’
‘tīyēṉ’ eṉaṟpālatu iyaṟcīrveṇṭaḷai piḻaiyāmaip poruṭṭākat ‘tiyēṉ’ eṉa neṭil kuṟuki niṉṟatu. (naṉ.155)
kūṭṭi eḻūutal -
eḻu - taṉviṉai; eḻūu - piṟaviṉai. kūṭṭi eḻūutalāvatu cērttu eḻupputal. itaṉāl taṉviṉaiyaip piṟaviṉai yākkutaṟ poruṭṭāka aḷapeṭai payaṉpaṭuttavāṟu pulaṉām. itu collicai yaḷapeṭaiyiṉpāṟpaṭum.	(to. e. 6 nac.)
keṭutal -
puṇarcci vikāram mūṉṟaṉuḷ keṭutalum oṉṟu.
	e-ṭu :	maram + vēr = maravēr
nilaimoḻiyīṟṟu makaram keṭṭamai ivvikāramām.			(naṉ. 154)
kēṇmiyā : collamaippu -
kēḷ eṉṟa muṉṉilai viṉaippakutiyoṭu ‘miyā’ eṉṟa uraiyacaiccol cērak kēṇmiyā eṉṟākum eṉpar tolkāppiṉār.
‘kēṇm’ eṉṟa colloṭu, yā eṉṉum (muṉṉilai) acaiccol, iṭaiyē ikaram tōṉṟappeṟṟuc cēra, kēṇmiyā eṉṟākum eṉpar ikkāla āyvāḷar.	(e. ā. pak. 36)
kainnoṭi aḷavu -
kaṭṭaiviralai naṭuviralil ūṉṟi iraṇṭaṉaiyum muṟukki oliyeḻupputaṟkaṇ nikaḻum kāla iṭaiveḷiyum, eḻuttiṉ aḷavākiya orumāttirai kāla iṭaiveḷiyum oṉṟākum. ivvāṟu olieḻupputaṟkaṇ, niṉaittamāttiraiyāṉē kālmāttiraiyum, kaṭṭaiviralai naṭuviralil ūṉṟiyavaḻi arai māttiraiyum, muṟukkiyavaḻi mukkāl māttiraiyum, ōcai eḻuppiyavaḻi oru māttiraiyum ākiya kālakkaḻivu nikaḻum eṉpa.
noṭittaltoḻilil piṟanta ōcaiyatu tōṟṟak kēṭṭuk kālakkaḻivum, a eṉap piṟanta ōcaiyatu tōṟṟak kēṭṭuk kālakkaḻivum okkum.	(to. e. 7 nac. urai)
kolyāṉai mutaliya viṉaittokaiyai oru collākak kōṭal -
mukkālattukkum potuvāṉa viṉaittokaiyai oru kālattiṟkuriya peyareccat toṭarāka virittal kuṉṟak kūṟalām eṉṟu karuti, viṉaittokaiyai nilaimoḻi varumoḻiyākap pakuttavaḻit tokaipporuḷ citaiyum eṉṟa karuttāṉ āciriyar viṉaittokaiyaip ‘puṇariyal nilaiyiṭai uṇarat tōṉṟātu’ eṉa, orucol nīrmaiya tākavē koṇṭamaiyiṉ, nac. viṉaittokaiyai oru collākak kāṭṭum eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷoṭu kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.	(to. e. 24 nac.)
koḷ eṉṉum vikuti -
koḷ eṉpatu viṉaippayaṉ viṉaimutalaic ceṉṟaṭaita lākiya taṟporuṭṭup poruṭkaṇ vanta vikuti. itaṉai vaṭanūlār ‘āṟpaṉēpatam’ eṉpa.
	e-ṭu :	ceytukoṇṭāṉ.
itaṉkaṇ, ceytalākiya viṉaiyiṉ payaṉ eḻuvāyākiya āṇpāṟporuḷaiyē ceṉṟaṭaital uṇarttappaṭṭatu.		(cū.vi. pak. 41)
kō eṉum col puṇarumāṟu -
kō eṉṟa ōkāra īṟṟuccol urupupuṇarccikkaṇṇum poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum oṉcāriyaipeṟṟu varumoḻi yoṭu puṇarum. oṉcāriyai piṟkālattu ‘ṉ’ cāriyai āyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu 	kō + oṉ + ai = kōoṉai; kō + oṉ + kai = kōoṉkai
ōkāraīṟṟup peyar okaramākiya eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭai peṟutalum, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkup puṇartalum vēṟṟumaip puṇarccikkaṇ uṇṭu.
e-ṭu : kō + kaṭumai = kōokkaṭumai
kō eṉpatu aracaṉaik kuṟikkum uyartiṇaipporuḷatu āyiṉum, kō vantatu eṉṟāṟ pōla aḵṟiṇai viṉai yoṭu muṭitaliṉ, collāṉ aḵṟiṇaiyām. aracu, amaiccu, tūtu, purōcu, oṟṟu mutalāyiṉavum aṉṉa.	(to. e. 292, 293 nac. urai)
kōṭṭunūṟum mañcaḷum kūṭiyavaḻip piṟanta cevvaṇṇam -
cuṇṇāmpum mañcaḷum iraṇṭaṟak kūṭiyavaḻiyē cenniṟam tōṉṟuvatu pōla, neṭilum kuṟilum iṇaintu oṉṟākak kūṭiya kūṭṭattup piṟantu, piṉṉar appiḷavupaṭā ōcaiyai aḷapeṭai eṉṟu tol. vēṇṭiṉār eṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar.  (e. 6 nac. urai) aḷapeṭai iraṇṭu māttiraiyum oru māttiraiyumākap pirittu acai koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, aḷapeṭaik kuṟil alaku peṟāta nilaiyumuṇṭu ātalāṉum, aḷapeṭaikkaṇ neṭilum kuṟilum viralum viralum cēraniṉṟāṟpōla aḷapeṭukkum eṉpatē tol. karuttātal peṟappaṭukiṟatu.	(e. ā. pak. 45,46).
kō, mā eṉṉum coṟkaḷ yakaram peṟutal -
kō, mā eṉpavaṟṟu muṉ uyir variṉ, vakaramē aṉṟi yakaramum uṭampaṭumeyyām.
	varumāṟu :	kō + il = (kōvil) kōyil
		mā + iru (ñālam) = māyiru (ñālam)
(mu.vī. puṇa. 25)

kōṉ eṉṟa col puṇarumāṟu -
kōṉ eṉṟa iyaṟpeyar ‘tantai’ eṉṟa muṟaippeyaroṭum, ‘makaṉ’ eṉṟa muṟaiyil kuṟittu varu kiḷaviyāka varum ēṉaiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷoṭum poruntumvaḻi, ṉakaraīṟṟa iyaṟ peyarkaḷiṉ ciṟappuviti peṟāmal, aḵṟiṇai iyaṟpeyar kaḷukkuriya potuvitiyāṉ iyalpāka muṭiyum.
	varumāṟu :	kōṉ + tantai = kōṉṟantai
		kōṉ + koṟṟaṉ = kōṉ koṟṟaṉ				(to. e. 351 nac.)
ṅa
ṅakaram mutal ātal -
ṅaṉam eṉpatu iṭattiṉaiyum taṉmaiyiṉaiyum uṇarttum palaporuḷ orucollāy variṉum, taṉittu varum taṉmaiyataṉṟi, muṭavaṉ kōl ūṉṟi vantāṟpōlac cuṭṭum viṉāvum ākiya iṭaiccoṟkaḷai muṉṉiṭṭu varutalāṉ ‘vaḻi’ eṉṟum, ēṉaiya meykaḷ pōla mutalākā maiyiṉ ‘avvoṭu’ eṉṉātu  ‘avvai oṭṭi,  eṉṟum, oruvāṟṟāṉ mutalātaliṉ iḻivu ciṟappāka ‘ṅavvum’ eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.	(naṉ. 106 caṅkara.)
ṅakaram moḻimutal ākāmai -
naṉṉūlār ‘cuṭṭiyā...... mutalākummē’ eṉṟu ṅakaram, a i u eṉṉum mūṉṟu cuṭṭum - yāviṉāvum - ekaraviṉāvum- eṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉvaḻi, akarattoṭu moḻimutalākum eṉṟu kūṟiyamai poruntātu. moḻikku mutalām eḻuttu ivai, īṟāvaṉa ivai eṉa īṇṭuk karuvi ceytatu, mēl puṇariyalil, nilaimoḻi īṟu varumoḻi mutalōṭu iyaiyap puṇarkkum poruṭṭaṉṟē? avvāṟu puṇarttaṟku iyaipillāta ṅakaramum, aṅṅaṉam - iṅṅaṉam - uṅṅaṉam - yāṅṅaṉam - eṅṅaṉam - eṉa ivvāṟu moḻikku mutalām eṉṟal payaṉil kūṟṟām. aṉṟiyum, aṅku - āṅku - yāṇṭu- yāṇṭaiyatu - aṉṉa - eṉṉa - eṉṟāṟpōlum ivvoṟ ṟukkaḷum (ṅ, ṇ, ṉ eṉpaṉa) moḻikku mutalām eṉṟal vēṇṭutaliṉ, avarkkum atu karuttaṉṟu eṉpatu.		(i.vi. eḻut. 27 urai)
ca
cakara akaram moḻimutaṟkaṇ vārāmai -
cattāṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkaṟpāla col cettāṉ eṉṟu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. iccol teluṅkil cacceṉu eṉavum, kaṉṉaṭattil cattaṉu eṉavum, malaiyāḷattil cattu eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ivaṟṟāl cakaraakaram paṇṭu cakaraekarac cāyaiyil tamiḻil olittirukkum eṉṟu uyttuṇaralām.
cari camaḻppu caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi caḷi cakaṭu caṭṭai cavaḷi cavi caraṭu cantu cataṅkai caḻakku mutaliya coṟkaḷ tolkāppiya ṉār kālattu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭila eṉpatu aṟiyattakkatu.	(e. ā. pak. 66, 67)
cakaram moḻimutal ātal -
cari camaḻppu caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi caḷi cakaṭu caṭṭai cavaḷi cavi caraṭu cantu cataṅkai caḻakku mutaliyaṉa vaḻakku ceyyuḷ eṉum īriṭattum varum cakaramutaṉ moḻikaḷ.	(naṉ. 105 mayilai.)
cakara mutaṉmoḻi -
‘cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē, a ai oḷaeṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē’ eṉpatu mutalāka iṉṉōraṉṉa cila eḻuttuk kaḷai moḻikku mutalākā eṉa āciriyar tol. vilakkiṉā reṉiṉ, ivvaṭamoḻikaḷum ticaiccoṟkaḷum akkālattu ivvāṟu tamiḻiṉkaṇ payiṉṟu vārāmai paṟṟi eṉka.	(naṉ. 106 civañā.)
caṅkatam mutalākiya nāṉku -
col, caṅkatam eṉavum pākatam eṉavum canukkirakam eṉavum avappirañcaṉam eṉavum nāṉku vakaippaṭum.	(mu.vī. moḻi. 28)
cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉaviṭattup pirappaṅkōl ākāmai -
cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉaviṭattup pirappaṅkōl ākātu; atu pōla, ikaraukaraṅkaḷ kuṟukiṉaviṭattum avai uyirākaṟ pālaṉa. avaṟṟaip puṇarccivēṟṟumaiyum poruḷ vēṟṟumaiyum nōkki vēṟu eḻuttu eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉār.	(to. e. 2 iḷa. urai)
ikara ukaram kuṟukiniṉṟaṉa, vikāravakaiyāṉ puṇarcci vēṟu paṭutaliṉ. ivaṟṟai iṅṅaṉam kuṟiyiṭṭu āḷutal ellāruk kum oppa muṭintatu. cantaṉak kōl kuṟukiṉāl pirappaṅkōl ākātu; atu pōla uyiratu kuṟukkamum uyirēyām.	(2 nac. urai)
aikārakkuṟukkam mutaliyaṉa oru kāraṇam paṟṟik kuṟukiṉa vākaliṉ, ciṟumaram peruttuḻiyum perumaram ciṟuttuḻiyum vēṟoru maram ākātavāṟu pōla, vēṟeḻuttu eṉappaṭā.		(cū.vi. pak. 30).
canti muṭivu mūṉṟē -
tōṉṟiya cantiyum, tirinta cantiyum keṭṭa cantiyum eṉac canti mūṉṟām.
yāṉaikkōṭu eṉpatu tōṉṟiya canti; maṭkuṭam eṉpatu tirinta canti; maravēr eṉpatu keṭṭa canti. (ivaṟṟuḷ muṟaiyē kakaramey tōṉṟiyavāṟum, ṇakaram ṭakaramāyt tirintavāṟum, makaram keṭṭavāṟum kāṇka.)
cantiyiṉai nāl eṉa iyalpucantiyum kūṭṭic cilar colla, innūluṭaiyār iyalpucantiyai nīkkiyatu eṉṉaiyō eṉiṉ, iyalpucantikaḷil mikkum tirintum keṭṭum varuvaṉa illai yātaliṉ, muṭikkavēṇṭum cantikaḷ illāmalē (illāmaiyālē eṉpatu poruḷ) nīkkiṉār.	(nēmi. eḻut. 12 urai)
cantiyakkaram -
akarattiṉ muṉṉar ikaramum yakaramum tammuḷ ottu eytiṉ ai eṉṉum neṭṭeḻuttu olikkum. akarattuṭaṉ ukaramum vakaramum tammuḷ ottu orutaṉmaiyavāka eytiṉ oḷa eṉṉum neṭ ṭeḻuttu olikkum.
‘moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyāta taṉaiyum muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal’ eṉṉum uttiyāṉ, akarak kūṟum ikarakkūṟum tammuḷ ottu ekaram olikkum; akarak kūṟum ukarak kūṟum tammuḷ ottu okaram olikkum eṉak koḷka.
iṉi iccūttirattiṟku ivvāṟu poruḷ koḷḷātu, tamiḻ nūṟpayiṟci oṉṟumēyuṭaiyār eḻuttuppōli uṇarttiṟṟu eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭu, 
	a i = ai; ay = ai; kai = kai; kay = kai
	a u = oḷa; av = oḷa; kau = kau; kav = kau
eṉa utāraṇam kāṭṭuvar. avvāṟu poruḷkoṇṭu utāraṇam kāṭṭumāṟṟāl perumpayaṉ iṉmaiyāṉum, vaṭanūloṭu māṟupaṭum ātalāṉum atu poruntātu eṉka.	(naṉ. 125 civañā.)
‘cār’: puṇarumāṟu -
cār eṉṉum marappeyar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi iṉamel leḻuttu mikum.
e-ṭu : cārṅkōṭu, cārñcetiḷ, cārntōl, cārmpū
varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ kāḻ eṉṟa col variṉ, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam mikkup puṇarum.
	cār + kāḻ = cārkkāḻ eṉa varum (to. e. 363, 364 nac.)
cārpu  vēṟupeyarkaḷ -
pullal eṉiṉum, cārtal eṉiṉum, puṇartal eṉiṉum, cārpeṉṉum oruporuṭ kiḷavi.	(mu. vī. eḻut. 23)

cārpeḻuttiṉ iṭamum muyaṟciyum -
cārpeḻuttukkaḷ  tattam mutaleḻuttukkaḷ tōṉṟum iṭamē tamakkup piṟappiṭamāy avaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟattukkuriya muyaṟciyē tam tōṟṟattukkum muyaṟciyāyp piṟakkum. ākavē, avai tamakkeṉat taṉippiṟappiṭamō muyaṟciyō uṭaiyaṉa alla.	(naṉ. 87)
cārpeḻuttu -
taṉittāṉum kakaraoṟṟu mutaliyaṉa pōla akaramoṭu civaṇi yāṉum iyaṅkum iyalpiṉṟi, oru moḻiyaic cārntu varutalē tamakku ilakkaṇamākavuṭaiya kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum cārpeḻuttām. cārntuvaral marapiṉaiyuṭaiya mūṉṟum akaram pōlat taṉittu niṟṟal āṟṟāmaiyiṉ, neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉuḷ peṟap paṭā ātaliṉ, ivai ‘eḻuttu ōraṉṉa’ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.	(cū. vi. pak. 18, 19)
tamakkeṉat taṉitta piṟappiṭamiṉṟit tām cārnta eḻuttiṉ piṟappē piṟappiṭamākat tōṉṟum kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiya lukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟē cārpeḻuttām.	(to. e. 101 nac.)
uyirum meyyumākiya mutaleḻuttaic cārntu avaṟṟiṉ iṭamākap piṟappaṉa cārpeḻuttukkaḷām. avaiyāvaṉa uyirmey, āytam, uyiraḷapeṭai, oṟṟaḷapeṭai, kuṟṟiya likaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, aikārak kuṟukkam, oḷakārak kuṟukkam, makarak kuṟukkam, āytak kuṟukkam eṉṟa pattām.	(naṉ. 60)
uyirmey uyirum meyyum kūṭip piṟattalāṉum, āytam uyirpōla orōvaḻi alaku peṟṟum meypōla orōvaḻi alaku peṟātum orupuṭai ottu avaṟṟiṉiṭaiyē cārntu varutalāṉum, ēṉaiyavai tattam mutaleḻuttiṉ tiripu vikārattāl piṟattalāṉum cārpeḻuttāyiṉa. (naṉ. 60 caṅ.)
tammoṭu tām cārntum, iṭaṉ cārntum, iṭaṉum paṟṟuk kōṭum cārntum vikārattāl varutaliṉ cārpeḻut tāyiṉa.	(mayilai.)
cārpeḻuttu oṉpatu eṉṟal tolkāppiyaṉārkku uṭaṉpāṭē -
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum cārpeḻuttu eṉṟa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum, ēṉaiya uyirmey uyiraḷapeṭai oṟṟaḷapeṭai aikārakkuṟukkam oḷakārakkuṟukkam makarak kuṟukkam eṉṟa āṟaṉaiyum piṉṉar oruvāṟṟāṉ taḻuvutalāṉum, (mutalum cārpum aṉṟi) mūṉṟāvatoru pakuti iṉṟātalāṉum, mutaleḻuttām taṉmai ivvoṉpataṟkum iṉmaiyāṉum, avai cārpil tōṉṟutaluṭaimaiyāṉum, ivvāṟaṉaiyum avaṟṟuṭaṉ talaippeytu ‘oṉpatum cārpiṉ pāla’ eṉṟār. ,	(i. vi. eḻut. 5 urai).
cārpeḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappu -
cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟum, tām cārntu tōṉṟum talaimai yeḻuttukkaḷiṉ vaḷiyicai - viṉaikkaḷa muyaṟci – piṟappiyalpu kaḷōṭu oruṅkottu, tattam iyalpoṭu kūṭi avviraṇṭu taṉmaiyum otta tōṟṟattōṭu uruvākip piṟakkum.
	varumāṟu : miyā eṉpataṉkaṇ niṟkum kuṟṟiyalikaram, makarattiṉatu piṟappiṭamākiya iyainta itaḻai yum yakarattiṉ piṟappiṭamākiya aṭinā aṇṇat taiyum cārntu, taṉatu iyalpu tōṉṟap piṟakkum.
nākaritu eṉṉum coṟkaḷuḷ niṟkum kuṟṟiyalukaram, taṉatu paṟṟukkōṭākiya kakaramey piṟappiṭattaiyum cārpākiya akarattiṉ piṟappiṭattaiyum cārntu, taṉatu iyalpu tōṉṟap piṟakkum.
aḵtu eṉṉum coṟkaṇniṟkum āytam, akarattiṟkum takara meykkum uriya aṇṇam - pal- nā- ākiya uṟuppukkaḷiṉ toḻilāṉ avaṟṟaic cārntu taṉatu iyalpu tōṉṟap piṟakkum.	(to.e.101. ca.pāla.)
cārpeḻuttukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai paṟṟiya ilakkaṇa nūlār koḷkai -
